Abstract: Green parafermions, originally introduced by Green and extended by Greenberg and Messiah through trilinear and relative trilinear commutation relations beyond Bose-Fermi statistics, are generally regarded as mathematical curiosities without physical realization. We show that these paraparticles can in fact emerge as composite excitations in a broad class of condensed-matter systems undergoing spontaneous symmetry breaking with type-B Goldstone modes. The key ingredient is the introduction of auxiliary Majorana fermions defined on emergent unit cells produced by partial translational-symmetry breaking. When the auxiliary Majoranas are treated as physical degrees of freedom, the resulting Green parafermion states (up to a projection operator) correspond to flat-band excitations, whose creation and annihilation operators satisfy the trilinear algebra. When they are regarded as fictitious, the same construction explains the appearance of exponentially many degenerate ground states and reveals a surprising correspondence between Green parafermions and self-similar geometric objects, such as the golden spiral. Explicit realizations are demonstrated for the ferromagnetic spin-1 biquadratic model and the ferromagnetic $\rm {SU}(2)$ flat-band Tasaki model, showing that condensed-matter systems with type-B Goldstone modes provide a natural setting for Green parafermions as emergent, possibly observable quasiparticles.

</details>


### [83] [Symmetry group factorization and unitary equivalence among Temperley-Lieb integrable models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21242)
*Huan-Qiang Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了Temperley-Lieb可积模型中两个重要序列之间的隐藏联系：自对偶点的q态量子Potts模型和交错SU(n)自旋s链，以及它们之间的对称群分解和幺正等价关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Temperley-Lieb可积模型中不同序列之间的内在联系，特别是量子Potts模型和交错SU(n)自旋链之间的隐藏对应关系，以揭示这些看似不同模型之间的深层统一性。

Method: 通过分析对称群分解和幺正等价性，建立了q态量子Potts模型与交错SU(n)自旋s链之间的对应关系。当q分解为q₁和q₂时，证明模型具有S_{q₁}×S_{q₂}对称群；当n分解为n₁和n₂时，证明交错SU(n)自旋链具有SU(n₁)×SU(n₂)对称群。

Result: 发现量子Potts模型与交错SU(n)自旋链之间存在幺正等价性：铁磁（反铁磁）交错SU(n)自旋s链与反铁磁（铁磁）q态量子Potts模型（q=n²）幺正等价，只需将交错链的尺寸加倍。这种等价性揭示了不同TL可积模型之间的本质同一性。

Conclusion: 论文建立了Temperley-Lieb可积模型中两个重要序列之间的系统对应关系，揭示了对称群分解和幺正等价性的普遍规律，为理解不同可积模型之间的深层联系提供了新视角。

Abstract: It is shown that there is a hidden connection between the two well-studied sequences of the Temperley-Lieb (TL) integrable models -- the $q$-state quantum Potts (QP) models at the self-dual points and the staggered ${\rm SU}(n)$ spin-$s$ chains with $n=2s+1$ ($s \ge 1$), in addition to the uniform ${\rm SU}(2)$ spin-$1/2$ Heisenberg model. For each sequence, symmetry group factorization arises, in the sense that if $q$ is factorized into $q_1$ and $q_2$, then the $q$-state QP model is unitarily equivalent to a combined QP model with the symmetry group ${\rm S}_{q_1} \times {\rm S}_{q_2}$ or if $n$ is factorized into $n_1$ and $n_2$, then the staggered ${\rm SU}(n)$ spin-$s$ chain with the symmetry group ${\rm SU}(n)$ is unitarily equivalent to a combined staggered ${\rm SU}(n_1) \times {\rm SU}(n_2)$ spin chain with the symmetry group ${\rm SU}(n_1) \times {\rm SU}(n_2)$, valid for both ferromagnetic (FM) and antiferromagnetic (AF) cases. Moreover, the FM (AF) staggered ${\rm SU}(n)$ spin-$s$ chain is unitarily equivalent to the AF (FM) $q$-state QP model with $q=n^2$, as long as the size of the AF (FM) staggered ${\rm SU}(n)$ spin-$s$ chain is doubled. A combination of the two distinct types of unitary equivalences yields a family of models such that they are essentially identical, but appear in different guises. Some physical implications for unitary equivalence among different TL integrable models are clarified.

</details>


### [84] [Non-invertible symmetries and boundary conditions for the transverse-field Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21290)
*Huan-Qiang Zhou,Qian-Qian Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文为非可逆Kramers-Wannier对偶对称性构造了在自对偶点横向场伊辛模型中的实现方案，通过引入额外自由度将非平移不变哈密顿量转化为扩充希尔伯特空间中的平移不变哈密顿量，得到了与Tambara-Yamagami Z2融合范畴相似的晶格版本融合规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究横向场伊辛模型在自对偶点的非可逆Kramers-Wannier对偶对称性，解决不同边界条件下哈密顿量构造问题，确保所有边界条件在热力学极限下给出收敛结果。

Method: 通过向希尔伯特空间引入额外自由度，将原始空间中的非平移不变哈密顿量转化为扩充空间中的平移不变哈密顿量。将恒等算子、Z2对称算子和非可逆KW对偶对称算子提升到扩充希尔伯特空间，针对四种环面边界条件构造对应算子。

Result: 成功构造了晶格版本的融合规则，与Tambara-Yamagami Z2融合范畴相似。对于周期和反周期边界条件，再现了Seiberg等人的结果；对于对偶扭曲边界条件，揭示了存在差异。

Conclusion: 该构造满足所有边界条件在热力学极限下收敛的基本物理要求，为非可逆对称性在晶格模型中的实现提供了系统框架，揭示了不同边界条件下融合规则的微妙差异。

Abstract: Non-invertible Kramers-Wannier (KW) duality symmetries are constructed for the transverse-field Ising model (TFIM) at the self-dual point under various boundary conditions (BCs), as long as the resultant Hamiltonian commutes with the ${\rm Z}_2$ symmetry operator. This is achieved by introducing extra degrees of freedom into the Hilbert space, in order to turn a non-translation-invariant Hamiltonian in the original Hilbert space into a translation-invariant Hamiltonian in the augmented Hilbert space. One may lift the trivial identity operator, the ${\rm Z}_2$ symmetry operator and the non-invertible KW duality symmetry operator to their counterparts in the augmented Hilbert space, valid for each of four types of toroidal BCs. As it turns out, they yield a lattice version of fusion rules, which bears a resemblance to the Tambara-Yamagami ${\rm Z}_2$ fusion category. Our construction is thus consistent with the basic physical requirement that all possible BCs should yield a converging result in the thermodynamic limit. In particular, the lattice versions of fusion rules, constructed by Seiberg, Seifnashri and Shao [SciPost Phys. \textbf{16}, 154 (2024)], are reproduced for periodic and anti-periodic BCs, but a discrepancy is revealed for duality-twisted BCs.

</details>


### [85] [Drinfeld Center as Quantum State Monodromy over Bloch Hamiltonians around Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22029)
*Hisham Sati,Urs Schreiber*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文证明Drinfeld中心融合范畴$\mathcal{Z}(\mathrm{Vec}_G)$的融合规则可以描述分数拓扑绝缘体材料中布里渊区点缺陷附近的拓扑序，建立了晶格模型任意子与材料拓扑序之间的数学联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，Drinfeld中心融合范畴$\mathcal{Z}(\mathrm{Vec}_G)$主要用于描述晶格模型中的任意子。本文旨在探索该数学结构是否也能描述真实材料（特别是分数拓扑绝缘体）中的拓扑序，特别是在布里渊区点缺陷附近的情况。

Method: 通过证明$\mathcal{Z}(\mathrm{Vec}_G)$在布里渊区穿孔圆盘上局部反映了具有基本群$G$的布洛赫哈密顿量参数空间中量子态的monodromy（拓扑序）。具体方法涉及拓扑绝缘体的参数空间理论和融合范畴的数学对应。

Result: 成功证明了$\mathcal{Z}(\mathrm{Vec}_G)$的融合规则确实可以描述分数拓扑绝缘体材料中布里渊区点缺陷附近的拓扑序，建立了晶格模型任意子与材料拓扑序之间的精确数学对应关系。

Conclusion: Drinfeld中心融合范畴$\mathcal{Z}(\mathrm{Vec}_G)$不仅适用于晶格模型中的任意子描述，也能有效描述真实分数拓扑绝缘体材料中的拓扑序，特别是在布里渊区点缺陷附近，为理解材料中的拓扑相提供了新的数学框架。

Abstract: The Drinfeld center fusion category $\mathcal{Z}(\mathrm{Vec}_G)$ famously models anyons in certain lattice models. Here we demonstrate how its fusion rules may also describe topological order in fractional topological insulator materials, in the vicinity of point defects in the Brillouin zone.
  Concretely, we prove that $\mathcal{Z}(\mathrm{Vec}_G)$ reflects, locally over a punctured disk in the Brillouin zone, the monodromy (topological order) of gapped quantum states over the parameter space of Bloch Hamiltonians whose classifying space has fundamental group $G$.

</details>


### [86] [Universally Diverging Grüneisen Ratio of Holographic Quantum Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21320)
*Jun-Kun Zhao,Enze Lv,Wei Li,Li Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用全息对偶研究场致量子临界点，发现新的普适类，格临爱森比发散行为与重费米子材料实验一致


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子临界性是强关联电子系统的重要特征，全息对偶为研究这些系统提供了有力框架。本文旨在利用这一方法研究场致量子临界点，特别是与重费米子材料实验观测相关的现象。

Method: 采用全息对偶方法，在爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-陈-西蒙斯理论中研究具有动力学指数z=3的场致量子临界点。通过分析该系统的热力学性质来探索其临界行为。

Result: 发现了一个新的普适类，特别识别出格临爱森比以普适标度~T^{-2/3}发散的行为。这一行为与重费米子材料CeRh6Ge4的最新实验结果高度吻合。

Conclusion: 研究结果推进了对金属量子临界性的理解，并突显了全息对偶作为研究关联量子物质的有力工具的潜力。理论预测与实验观测的一致性验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Quantum criticality is a hallmark of strongly correlated electron systems, as seen in heavy-fermion materials and high-temperature superconductors. Holographic duality provides a powerful framework to investigate these systems by translating them into weakly coupled classical gravity living in one higher dimension. Here, we harness this approach to study a field-induced quantum critical point with dynamical exponent $z=3$ in Einstein-Maxwell-Chern-Simons theory. Our analysis of its thermodynamic properties reveals a new universality class. Notably, we identify a diverging Grüneisen ratio with universal scaling $\sim T^{-2/3}$, a behavior that closely mirrors recent experiments on the heavy-fermion material CeRh$_6$Ge$_4$. These findings advance our understanding of metallic quantum criticality and highlight the potential of holographic duality as a tool for studying correlated quantum matters.

</details>


### [87] [Colossal Terahertz Magnetoresistance from Magnetic Polarons in EuZn$_2$P$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21423)
*E. Marulanda,M. Dutra,N. M. Kawahala,E. D. Stefanato,G. G. Vasques,J. Munevar,M. A. Avila,F. G. G. Hernandez*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过太赫兹光谱学揭示了EuZn₂P₂中磁极化子对低能电动力学的关键作用，发现太赫兹频率下的巨磁阻效应比直流极限值大三倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁极化子可在磁性半导体中产生巨磁阻效应，但其太赫兹电动力学特性尚未得到充分探索。本研究旨在通过太赫兹光谱技术研究Eu基Zintl反铁磁体EuZn₂P₂中的磁极化子行为。

Method: 采用磁太赫兹光谱技术对EuZn₂P₂进行测量，分析其低频电导率行为，研究磁极化子从孤立态到重叠态的演化过程，以及极化子弛豫时间与温度和磁场的依赖关系。

Result: 低频电导率表现出显著的非德鲁德行为，极化子弛豫时间在奈尔温度达到最大值且具有强磁场依赖性。太赫兹频率下的磁阻效应达到约90%（1.5 THz），比零频率极限值大约三倍。

Conclusion: 磁极化子强烈主导低能电动力学，太赫兹光谱对关联磁性半导体中的极化子磁输运具有高度敏感性，为理解磁极化子驱动的巨磁阻效应提供了新视角。

Abstract: Magnetic polarons can generate colossal magnetoresistance in magnetic semiconductors, yet their terahertz electrodynamics remain largely unexplored. Here we report magneto-terahertz spectroscopy of the Eu-based Zintl antiferromagnet EuZn$_2$P$_2$. The low-frequency conductivity shows pronounced non-Drude behavior consistent with an evolution from isolated to overlapping magnetic polarons upon cooling. The polaron relaxation time reaches a maximum at the Néel temperature and exhibits a strong magnetic-field dependence. This polaron-driven reshaping of the conductivity leads to a strongly frequency-dependent magnetoresistance that becomes colossal in the terahertz range, reaching about 90 % at 1.5 THz, roughly three times larger than the zero-frequency limit value. These results demonstrate that magnetic polarons strongly govern the low-energy electrodynamics and highlight the sensitivity of terahertz spectroscopy to polaronic magnetotransport in correlated magnetic semiconductors.

</details>


### [88] [Band structure control in the altermagnetic candidate MnTe by temperature and strain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21455)
*Shin-ichi Kimura,Hironao Suwa,Kangle Yuan,Hiroshi Watanabe,Takuto Nakamura,Haan Kyul Yun,Myung-Hwa Jung*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过测量六方碲化锰的光导谱，发现太赫兹区域存在自旋分裂带至受主能级的吸收，该吸收峰的温度依赖性符合铁磁相变特征，但MnTe本身无净磁性，表明其具有交变磁性电子结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究六方碲化锰(MnTe)的光学性质，特别是太赫兹区域的吸收特性，以探索其独特的磁性行为。虽然MnTe没有净磁性，但其温度依赖性与铁磁相变相似，这促使研究者探究其潜在的电子结构机制。

Method: 测量六方碲化锰光学导谱的温度和应变依赖性，分析太赫兹区域的吸收特征。观察光学声子吸收的Fano-like反对称线形，并在负单轴压力下研究太赫兹峰的移动行为。

Result: 发现太赫兹吸收峰的温度依赖性与铁磁相变一致，但MnTe无净磁性。观察到光学声子与自旋分裂带相互作用导致的Fano-like线形。负单轴压力下太赫兹峰远离费米能级移动，与自旋分裂角减小的理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 太赫兹峰的行为明确表明MnTe具有交变磁性电子结构，其独特的温度依赖性和压力响应特性为理解无净磁性材料的自旋分裂现象提供了实验证据。

Abstract: The temperature and strain dependences of the optical conductivity spectrum of hexagonal manganese telluride (MnTe) were measured, revealing absorption in the terahertz (THz) region from spin-split bands to acceptor levels. The temperature dependence of the THz absorption peak is consistent with that of a ferromagnetic phase transition, even though MnTe exhibits no net magnetism. The temperature dependence was attributed to a change in the altermagnetic electronic structure. A Fano-like antisymmetric line shape in the optical phonon absorption was observed, which originates from the interaction between optical phonons and the spin-split bands. Additionally, under negative uniaxial pressure, the THz peak shifts away from the Fermi level (EF), suggesting that spin-splitting bands at energies away from EF, consistent with the theoretical prediction that the spin-splitting angle decreases. The observed behavior of the THz peak clearly shows that MnTe has the altermagnetic electronic structure.

</details>


### [89] [Dynamical spin correlations in kagome antiferromagnets: comparison of Abrikosov fermion and Schwinger boson approaches beyond mean field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21513)
*Daiki Sasamoto,Joji Nasu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究比较了Abrikosov费米子和Schwinger玻色子两种部分子方法在描述Kagome反铁磁海森堡模型自旋动力学方面的差异，发现两种方法给出不同的低能谱结构，且Schwinger玻色子方法需要考虑多体效应才能与实验观测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kagome反铁磁海森堡模型是量子自旋液体的重要候选体系，但其激发谱的性质（特别是自旋能隙和与分数化准粒子耦合的规范结构）仍存在争议。Abrikosov费米子和Schwinger玻色子两种部分子方法被广泛使用，但此前这些方法独立发展，不清楚它们在描述自旋动力学和规范结构方面的比较结果。

Method: 采用Abrikosov费米子和Schwinger玻色子两种部分子平均场理论，研究Kagome晶格上具有Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用的反铁磁海森堡模型（与herbertsmithite材料相关）的动力学自旋结构因子。在平均场近似基础上，进一步考虑了多体效应修正。

Result: Abrikosov费米子平均场理论得到的动力学自旋结构因子呈现穹顶状特征，其连续谱结构显著依赖于自旋液体ansatz的规范结构。Schwinger玻色子平均场理论在低能区产生凹向下的结构，与Abrikosov费米子方法的结果明显不同。超越平均场近似的多体效应显著降低了低能能隙并增强了低能谱权重，这与实验观测一致。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，Schwinger玻色子理论中考虑多体效应对于准确描述Kagome反铁磁体的低能自旋动力学至关重要。两种部分子方法给出了不同的低能谱结构，强调了规范结构在决定自旋液体激发谱中的重要性。

Abstract: Quantum spin liquids exhibit fractionalized spin excitations as a consequence of strong quantum many-body effects. The kagome antiferromagnetic Heisenberg model is a promising candidate for a quantum spin-liquid ground state; however, the nature of its excitation spectrum remains controversial, particularly regarding the presence of a spin gap and the gauge structure coupled to fractional quasiparticles. To address these issues, parton approaches have been extensively employed, where spin operators are represented in terms of fermionic or bosonic quasiparticles within the Abrikosov fermion and Schwinger boson frameworks. Thus far, these approaches have been pursued independently, and it has remained unclear how the results obtained from these frameworks compare, particularly with respect to the spin dynamics and gauge structure of the kagome antiferromagnet. Here, we investigate the dynamical spin structure factor of the antiferromagnetic Heisenberg model with a Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction on the kagome lattice, relevant to herbertsmithite, by employing both approaches. We find that the dynamical spin structure factor obtained from the Abrikosov fermion mean-field theory exhibits dome-shaped features, and that its continuum structure significantly depends on the gauge structure of the spin-liquid ansatz. On the other hand, the Schwinger boson mean-field theory yields a concave-down structure in the low-energy region, distinct from that obtained using the Abrikosov fermion approach. Moreover, incorporating many-body effects beyond the mean-field approximation substantially reduces the low-energy gap and enhances the low-energy spectral weight, consistent with experimental observations. Our results suggest the importance of many-body effects in the Schwinger boson theory for capturing the low-energy spin dynamics of kagome antiferromagnets.

</details>


### [90] [Tangent equations of motion for nonlinear response functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22137)
*Atsushi Ono*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出基于Gateaux导数的切线方程运动(TEOM)框架，直接通过实时动力学计算非线性响应函数，避免显式构造多点关联函数或数值不稳定的有限差分方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性响应函数在非线性输运和光学现象中至关重要，但高阶计算面临组合爆炸（阶乘增长）或严重数值误差的挑战，需要更高效稳定的计算方法。

Method: 基于函数空间中对外部场的Gateaux导数，建立切线方程运动(TEOM)的封闭层次结构。将TEOM与原始动力学方程并行传播，隔离每个微扰阶次，实现物理贡献的逐项分解。

Result: 1) 计算固态电子模型的五阶频率分辨响应函数；2) 在经典Duffing振子中获得高达49阶的非线性响应函数且精度可控；3) 光学电导率可直接计算且在零频率附近保持数值稳定。

Conclusion: TEOM提供了一种系统高效的计算非线性响应函数的框架，可无缝集成到现有实时演化方法中，为量子与经典动力学系统的非线性响应计算提供通用解决方案。

Abstract: Nonlinear response functions, formulated as multipoint correlation functions or Volterra kernels, encode the dynamical and spectroscopic properties of physical systems and underpin a wide range of nonlinear transport and optical phenomena. However, their evaluation rapidly becomes prohibitive at high orders because of combinatorial (often factorial) scaling or severe numerical errors. Here, we establish a systematic and efficient framework to compute nonlinear response functions directly from real-time dynamics, without explicitly constructing multipoint correlators or relying on numerically unstable finite-difference methods for order-resolved extraction. Our approach is based on the Gateaux derivative with respect to the external field in function space, which yields a closed hierarchy of tangent equations of motion (TEOM). Propagating the TEOM alongside the original dynamics isolates each perturbative order with high accuracy, providing a term-by-term decomposition of physical contributions. The computational cost scales exponentially with response order in the fully general setting and reduces to polynomial complexity when all perturbation directions are identical; both regimes avoid the factorial scaling of explicit multipoint-correlator evaluations. We demonstrate the power of TEOM by computing frequency-resolved fifth-order response functions for a solid-state electron model and by obtaining nonlinear response functions up to the 49th order with controlled accuracy in a classical Duffing oscillator. We further show that our time-evolution formulation allows optical conductivities to be evaluated directly while remaining numerically stable even near zero frequency. TEOM can be incorporated seamlessly into existing real-time evolution methods, yielding a general framework for computing nonlinear response functions in quantum and classical dynamical systems.

</details>


### [91] [Hall viscosity and putative quantum Hall states without positive-definite K-matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21794)
*Emanuele Di Salvo,Dirk Schuricht,Joost K. Slingerland,Mikael Fremling*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过定义环面上的量子霍尔态（K矩阵为(1 1 3)）并计算其霍尔粘度，探讨了这些作为Pfaffian和反Pfaffian相区分的态，以解释热霍尔电导率结果支持粒子-空穴对称Pfaffian拓扑序的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于解释实验观测到的热霍尔电导率结果，这些结果支持在朗道能级混合存在下的粒子-空穴对称Pfaffian拓扑序。K矩阵为(1 1 3)的态在球面上被提出作为与Pfaffian和反Pfaffian不同的相，需要在环面上进一步验证其性质。

Method: 通过在环面上定义这些量子霍尔态并计算其霍尔粘度。采用边界条件、模不变性和基态简并度要求来唯一确定波函数形式。推广了称为"单值匹配"的方法来实施这些要求，并在环面上的Laughlin态和分层态上验证了波函数和霍尔粘度的结果。

Result: 研究发现这些态表现出团簇形成的特征。反对称化操作不足以阻止团簇化。计算了这些态的Halperin版本和完全反对称化版本的霍尔粘度，发现它们依赖于几何结构和粒子数。

Conclusion: K矩阵为(1 1 3)的量子霍尔态在环面上表现出团簇形成的特征，其霍尔粘度具有几何和粒子数依赖性。这些结果有助于理解这些态作为Pfaffian和反Pfaffian相区分的候选者的性质，并揭示了实现这些态时面临的问题。

Abstract: We investigate putative quantum Hall effect states, labeled by their K-matrix equal to (1 1 3), by defining them on the torus and computing their Hall viscosity. Such states have been introduced on the sphere as a phase distinct from Pfaffian and anti-Pfaffian ones. This was done in order to explain certain results on thermal Hall conductivity in favor of particle-hole symmetric Pfaffian topological order in presence of Landau level mixing. The requirements of boundary conditions, modular invariance and ground state degeneracy are enough to uniquely fix the form of the proposed wave functions. We generalize a method to enforce them which we call monodromy matching and check our results on wave functions and Hall viscosity against realizations on the torus of Laughlin and hierarchical states. We highlight the issues in the realization of these states, which turn out to exhibit the formation of clusters. We show that the effect of anti-symmetrization on the system is not enough to prevent clustering; we compute the Hall viscosity for the Halperin version of these states and the fully anti-symmetrized one and we find them being dependent on the geometry and the particle number.

</details>


### [92] [An Exact Conjugation Identity for the Many-Body Wilson-Loop Beyond Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22217)
*Kai Watanabe*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文建立了多体重叠Wilson环的精确共轭恒等式W(-δ)=W(δ)*，证明了在U(1)通量穿线循环中，键二聚化参数δ反号时Wilson环互为复共轭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多体系统中Wilson环的对称性关系，特别是在存在键二聚化参数δ和U(1)通量穿线参数θ的情况下，探索Wilson环的精确数学关系及其物理意义。

Method: 建立复合反幺正映射作用于通量穿线哈密顿量族，实现(δ,θ)→(-δ,-θ)变换。在二聚化交错Hubbard环半填充模型中微观构造该映射，并使用密度矩阵重整化群验证共轭恒等式。

Result: 证明了精确的Wilson环共轭恒等式W(-δ)=W(δ)*，该恒等式在非钉扎能隙区域（Berry相位γ未对称量子化）仍然成立，并得出γ(-δ)=-γ(δ) (mod 2π)的推论。

Conclusion: Wilson环共轭关系适用于任何通量穿线哈密顿量族在键图案方向反转（合适的链接跳跃参数置换）与通量方向反转组合下闭合的晶格模型，揭示了多体系统中几何相位的普适对称性。

Abstract: We establish an exact Wilson-loop conjugation identity, $W(-δ)=W(δ)^*$, for the many-body overlap Wilson-loop $W(δ)$ accumulated along a $U(1)$ flux-threading (twist) cycle parametrized by $θ\in[0,2π]$, where $δ$ denotes the bond-dimerization parameter. A minimal sufficient condition is the existence of a composite antiunitary mapping acting on the flux-threaded Hamiltonian family that implements $(δ,θ)\mapsto(-δ,-θ)$. As a concrete demonstration, we construct such a mapping microscopically in a dimerized staggered Hubbard ring at half filling. We then verify the conjugation identity using the density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) for gapped, nondegenerate ground states along the twist cycle. Importantly, the identity persists in depinned gapped regimes where the Berry-phase $γ\equiv-\arg W$ is not symmetry-quantized; as a corollary, $γ(-δ)=-γ(δ)$ (mod $2π$). More generally, the same conjugation relation applies to any lattice model whose flux-threaded Hamiltonian family is closed under an orientation reversal of the bond pattern (a suitable permutation of link-hopping parameters) combined with a reversal of the flux orientation.

</details>


### [93] [Emergent relativistic symmetry from interacting fermions on the honeycomb bilayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22259)
*Zi Hong Liu,Lukas Janssen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过大规模量子蒙特卡洛模拟研究双层石墨烯在电荷中性点的相互作用相图，发现弱相互作用诱导二次能带接触点分裂为狄拉克点，强相互作用导致层极化电荷密度波绝缘相，相变属于Gross-Neveu-Ising普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双层石墨烯在电荷中性点的相互作用效应，特别是理解从非相互作用二次能带接触到相互作用诱导的狄拉克半金属，再到绝缘相的量子相变机制。

Method: 采用大规模量子蒙特卡洛模拟方法，研究相互作用自旋less费米子在双层蜂窝晶格上的相图，分析不同相互作用强度下的能带结构和相变行为。

Result: 弱到中等相互作用使每个二次能带接触点分裂为四个沿高对称方向的狄拉克点；强相互作用导致层极化电荷密度波绝缘相，自发破坏Z2层反演对称性；半金属-绝缘体量子相变是连续的，具有涌现的相对论对称性，临界指数与8个二分量狄拉克费米子的Gross-Neveu-Ising普适类一致。

Conclusion: 双层石墨烯在电荷中性点表现出丰富的相互作用相图，包括非相对论性半金属、狄拉克半金属和绝缘相，量子相变属于Gross-Neveu-Ising普适类，揭示了相互作用在二维材料中诱导拓扑相变的重要机制。

Abstract: We study the phase diagram of interacting spinless fermions on the honeycomb bilayer at charge neutrality using large-scale quantum Monte Carlo simulations. In the noninteracting limit, the low-energy spectrum features quadratically dispersing bands that touch at the corners of the hexagonal Brillouin zone. Weak to intermediate interactions induce a splitting of each of the quadratic band touching points into four Dirac points, located along high-symmetry directions of the reciprocal lattice. Strong interactions lead to the formation of a layer-polarized charge density wave, which spontaneously breaks the $\mathbb Z_2$ layer inversion symmetry and opens an insulating gap in the spectrum. We show that the semimetal-to-insulator quantum phase transition as a function of interaction is continuous and characterized by emergent relativistic symmetry. Our results for the values of the correlation-length exponent $ν$, the order-parameter anomalous dimension $η_φ$, and the fermion anomalous dimension $η_ψ$ agree with those of the theoretically predicted 2+1D Gross-Neveu-Ising universality class with eight two-component Dirac fermions within less than 5\%\ deviation. We also determine the crossover scale as a function of interaction strength between the nonrelativistic semimetal state at high temperatures, characterized by dynamical critical exponent $z = 2$, and the Dirac semimetal state at intermediates temperatures, characterized by $z=1$. Further reducing the temperature below the crossover scale at a fixed value of the interaction strength above the quantum critical point results in a classical ordering transition in the 2D Ising universality class.

</details>

### [94] [Competing skin effect and quasiperiodic localization in the non-Hermitian Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chain: Reentrant delocalization, spectral topology destruction, and entanglement suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20330)
*Souvik Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究探索了非厄米趋肤效应与Aubry-André-Harper准周期无序在具有非互易跃迁的一维SSH链中的相互作用，揭示了包含五种不同相态的完整相图，其中发现了竞争机制导致的重入部分退局域化现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是理解非厄米趋肤效应与准周期无序之间的相互作用，特别是在具有非互易跃迁的SSH链中。传统上，非厄米趋肤效应导致波函数局域在边界，而AAH无序导致安德森局域化，但两者如何竞争和共存尚不清楚。作者旨在揭示这种相互作用产生的丰富相行为，特别是寻找可能的新相态。

Method: 采用精确对角化、传递矩阵分析和解析相似变换论证相结合的方法。通过相位平均诊断和有限尺寸标度分析确认相图的鲁棒性。推导了修正的局域化边界解析表达式，并与数值李雅普诺夫指数计算进行比较。进一步分析了准周期无序对复谱环和点隙拓扑的影响，以及纠缠熵的变化。

Result: 绘制了完整的(λ, δ)相图，识别出五种不同机制：I)拓扑扩展体态、II)AAH局域化、III)趋肤局域化、IV)完全局域化、V)竞争机制（重入部分退局域化）。发现竞争机制中，中间准周期无序破坏方向性趋肤积累，最终导致所有态安德森局域化。推导了修正局域化边界λ_c(δ)=2√(v_eff w)，其中v_eff=√(v²-δ²)。证明准周期无序逐渐解开复谱环，在临界强度处破坏点隙拓扑，该临界强度不同于能带拓扑转变。发现趋肤效应将纠缠熵抑制至接近零值，而足够强的AAH无序部分恢复纠缠熵。

Conclusion: 该研究系统揭示了非厄米趋肤效应与准周期无序在SSH链中的复杂相互作用，发现了包含五种相态的丰富相图，特别是重入部分退局域化这一新机制。证明SSH子晶格结构对于产生这种五相景观至关重要，与广泛研究的非厄米AAH链形成对比。这些发现为非厄米系统中无序与拓扑的相互作用提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We investigate the interplay between the non-Hermitian skin effect and Aubry-André-Harper (AAH) quasiperiodic disorder in a one-dimensional Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) chain with nonreciprocal hopping. By exact diagonalization, transfer-matrix analysis, and an analytical similarity-transformation argument, we map the full ( , $δ$) phase diagram, where A is the AAH modulation strength and the nonreciprocity parameter. We identify five distinct regimes: ( ) topological with extended bulk, (II) AAH-localized, (III) skin-localized, (IV) fully localized, and a previously unreported (V) competition regime exhibiting reentrant partial delocalization, in which intermediate quasiperiodic disorder disrupts the directional skin accumulation before ultimately Anderson-localizing all states. Using phase-averaged diagnostics and finite-size scaling, we confirm that the reentrant regime is robust, characterized by a non-monotonic inverse participation ratio that sharpens with increasing system size. We derive an analytical expression for the modified localization boundary $λ_{c}(δ)=2\sqrt{v_{eff}w}$ with $v_{vff}=\sqrt{v^{2}-δ^{2}}$, which agrees with numerical Lyapunov exponent calculations. We further show that quasiperiodic disorder progressively unwinds the complex spectral loops, destroying the point-gap topology at a critical strength distinct from the band-topological transition ; that the skin effect suppresses entanglement entropy to near-zero values while sufficiently strong AAH disorder partially restores it ; and that the SSH sublattice structure absent in the widely studied non-Hermitian AAH chain is essential for producing the five-phase landscape, as demonstrated by direct comparison with the non-dimerized limit.

</details>


### [95] [Gate-tunable synthetic antiferromagnetism with nonrelativistic spin splitting in a graphene/MnS/graphene heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20458)
*Marko Milivojević,Martin Gmitra*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用石墨烯封装A型反铁磁半导体构建栅极可调合成反铁磁体，实现非相对论性自旋分裂，通过石墨烯实现高效自旋电子输运


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种易于合成、栅极可调的低维反铁磁器件平台，实现非相对论性自旋分裂以提升自旋电子输运效率

Method: 采用石墨烯/MnS/石墨烯异质结构，通过第一性原理计算和紧束缚模型分析，研究栅极调控对上下层石墨烯对称性的破坏效应

Result: 栅极调控打破异质结构对称性，诱导相反的铁磁邻近交换作用，在费米能级附近产生主导的非相对论性自旋分裂，表现为自旋分辨输运中的电导下降和窄能量窗口内的巨磁阻效应

Conclusion: 石墨烯/A型反铁磁异质结构为栅极操控的低维反铁磁器件提供了可行平台，结合了非相对论性自旋分裂特性，推动了自旋电子学器件发展

Abstract: We propose encapsulating type-A antiferromagnetic semiconductors between graphene layers to realize a gate-tunable synthetic antiferromagnet with nonrelativistic spin splitting, enabling efficient spintronic transport via graphene. Ab initio calculations and tight-binding models of graphene/MnS/graphene heterostructure reveal that gate-tuning of the heterostructure breaks top/bottom graphene equivalence, inducing opposite ferromagnetic proximity exchange that lifts spin degeneracy to yield nonrelativistic spin splitting at the Fermi level, dominating over relativistic effects. The induced effects manifest as conductance dips in spin-resolved transport through proximitized graphene nanoribbons, observable as giant magnetoresistance within a narrow energy window around the Fermi level. Our graphene/type-A antiferromagnetic heterostructure, a readily synthesizable platform incorporating antiferromagnets with nonrelativistic spin splitting, pave the way for gate-manipulated, low-dimensional antiferromagnetic devices.

</details>


### [96] [Twist-Induced Quantum Geometry Reconfiguration in Moiré Flat Bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20849)
*Yi-Chun Hung,Xiaoting Zhou,Arun Bansil*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究扭曲双层环流有序Kagome晶格(tb-LCK)中量子几何的扭曲诱导重构，发现扭曲抑制了单层Berry曲率的继承，揭示了moiré平带中非常规量子几何的新平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在扭曲双层石墨烯和过渡金属二硫化物等moiré系统中，moiré平带的量子几何通常反映单层的特性，Berry曲率源自同一谷的带边。但在具有复杂单层能带结构和对称性破缺的系统中，这种对应关系是否仍然存在尚不清楚。

Method: 使用紧束缚模型研究扭曲双层环流有序Kagome晶格(tb-LCK)，通过调节环流有序的相位，分析扭曲驱动的能带重构对量子几何的影响。

Result: 发现扭曲诱导的量子几何重构，扭曲抑制了单层Berry曲率的继承。这些效应归因于钒基Kagome材料固有的异常大的层间隧穿导致的强层间杂化，混合了能量上远离的态并重塑了量子几何。

Conclusion: 扭曲在tb-LCK中抑制了量子几何从单层的继承，确立了环流有序moiré系统作为探索moiré平带中非常规量子几何的有前景平台。通过钒基Kagome材料，所提出的系统在实验上具有可行性。

Abstract: The interplay between band topology, Berry curvature, and moiré flat bands lies at the heart of recent advances in quantum materials. In well-studied moiré systems such as twisted bilayer graphene and transition metal dichalcogenides, the quantum geometry of moiré flat bands typically reflects that of the monolayer, with Berry curvature originating from the band edge at the same valley. Whether this correspondence persists in systems with complex monolayer band structures and broken symmetries remains unclear. Here, we study twisted bilayers of loop-current-ordered kagome lattices (tb-LCK), which have been proposed in the context of vanadium-based kagome materials, using tight-binding models, and uncover a twist-induced reconfiguration of quantum geometry. By tuning the phase of the loop-current order, we identify the suppression of monolayer Berry curvature through twist-driven band reconstruction. We attribute these effects to strong interlayer hybridizations, enabled by the unusually large interlayer tunneling inherent to vanadium-based kagome materials, which mix energetically distant states and reshape quantum geometry. These results reveal that twist in tb-LCK suppresses quantum geometric inheritance from the monolayer, and establish loop-current-ordered moiré systems as promising platforms for exploring unconventional quantum geometry in moiré flat bands. We further comment on the experimental feasibility of the proposed system via vanadium-based kagome materials.

</details>


### [97] [Quantum Geometry of Moiré Flat Bands Beyond the Valley Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20852)
*Xiaoting Zhou,Yi-Chun Hung,Arun Bansil*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究开发了扭曲双层二聚体晶格紧束缚模型，揭示了层间杂化在平带量子几何中的作用，建立了超越谷范式的平带量子几何工程机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于谷的范式无法解释缺乏谷结构的新型材料中出现的奇异现象，如扭曲角可调平带数量。需要建立超越谷范式的机制来理解平带量子几何的起源。

Method: 开发并分析了扭曲双层二聚体晶格的紧束缚模型，重点关注层间杂化在产生平带量子几何中的作用。特别研究了扭曲骰子晶格和石墨烯异质双层中的亚晶格选择性层间隧穿。

Result: 亚晶格选择性层间隧穿在零能量处诱导出孤立的平带，其数量可由扭曲角调节。这些平带表现出有限的贝里曲率和拓扑绝缘体尺度的量子度量，这些性质通过层间杂化产生。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了在莫尔平带中诱导量子几何的机制，超越了传统谷范式。为扭曲双层二聚体晶格中的平带量子几何工程指明了路径，在氧化物异质结构、分子晶格和合成量子物质中具有潜在材料实现前景。

Abstract: Flat bands in moiré superlattices provide a fertile ground for correlated and topological phases, governed by their quantum geometric properties. While the valley-based paradigm captures key features in select materials, it breaks down in a growing class of systems lacking valley structure, where exotic phenomena such as twist-angle-tunable numbers of flat bands emerge. In this work, we develop and analyze tight-binding models for twisted heterobilayers of bipartite lattices, with a focus on the role of interlayer hybridization in generating flat-band quantum geometry. We demonstrate that sublattice-selective interlayer tunnelings in twisted dice lattice and graphene heterobilayers induce isolated flat bands at zero energy, whose number is tunable by the twist angle. Most importantly, these flat bands exhibit finite Berry curvature and a quantum metric of the Chern-insulator scale, generated through interlayer hybridization. This establishes a mechanism to induce quantum geometry in moiré flat bands beyond the valley paradigm. Our results chart a route to flat-band quantum geometry engineering in twisted bilayer bipartite lattices, with potential material realizations in oxide heterostructures, molecular lattices, and synthetic quantum matter.

</details>


### [98] [Chern Insulator in magnetic-doped two-dimensional semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20875)
*Dinh Loc Duong*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

Method: 在WSe2和WS2中掺杂V原子，利用强自旋轨道耦合诱导价带边缘附近的掺杂态与宿主能带发生能带反转

Abstract: We propose an approach to induce nontrivial bands with non-zero Chern numbers by utilizing strong spin-orbit coupling in transition metal dichalcogenides with dopants. We demonstrate that a doped state near the valence-band edge induces band inversion with the hybridized host band, leading to topologically non-trivial properties. Calculations for V-doped WSe2 and WS2 confirm this mechanism. The coexistence of magnetic order and nontrivial topology in these systems offers a promising platform for exploring the quantum anomalous Hall effect.

</details>


### [99] [Super-Klein tunneling in 2D Lorentzian-type barriers in graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20950)
*Alonso Contreras-Astorga,Francisco Correa,Luis Inzunza,Vit Jakubsky,Raul Valencia-Torres*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种二维石墨烯中自旋1/2狄拉克费米子模型，在可调电场下展示超克莱因隧穿现象，并利用超对称量子力学方法建立了与自由粒子动力学的内在联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究石墨烯中狄拉克费米子在可调电场下的量子隧穿行为，特别是探索超克莱因隧穿现象的理论基础及其与自由粒子动力学的内在联系。

Method: 采用二维自旋1/2狄拉克费米子模型，构建可连续插值的电场配置（从均匀静电洛伦兹势垒到分离静电散射体链），并运用超对称量子力学方法建立模型与自由粒子动力学的解析联系。

Result: 证明了超克莱因隧穿是该模型与自由粒子动力学内在联系的直接结果，揭示了模型的标度不变性以及特定能量粒子的势能不可见性，并讨论了实验实现的可能途径。

Conclusion: 该研究通过超对称量子力学框架为石墨烯中的超克莱因隧穿现象提供了优雅的解析理论解释，建立了可调电场下狄拉克费米子动力学与自由粒子系统的深刻联系，为实验观测提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We introduce a two-dimensional model of spin-1/2 Dirac fermions in graphene subjected to a highly tunable electric field, which exhibits super-Klein tunneling. The electric field can be continuously interpolated between two limiting configurations: a uniform electrostatic Lorentzian barrier with translational invariance and a chain of well-separated electrostatic scatterers. We demonstrate that super-Klein tunneling arises naturally as a direct consequence of the intrinsic connection of the model to free-particle dynamics, a relation that is established through methods of supersymmetric quantum mechanics, which provide an elegant and analytically tractable framework. Besides the mentioned super-Klein tunneling, scale invariance of the model and invisibility of the potential for particles of specific energy are revealed, and possible routes toward experimental realization are discussed.

</details>


### [100] [Equilibrium Magnetic Properties in Magnetic Nanoscrews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20958)
*Victoria Acosta-Pareja,Valeria M. A. Salinas,Omar J. Suarez,Attila Kákay,Jorge A. Otálora*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过微磁模拟研究铁磁纳米螺旋的平衡磁化，发现几何参数（曲率、扭转、偏心度）影响剩磁态和矫顽场，揭示双稳态磁行为和增强的矫顽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三维螺旋几何形状的磁性纳米结构（纳米螺旋）的磁化特性，探索几何参数（曲率、扭转、偏心度）对磁性能的影响，为三维纳米磁学应用提供基础。

Method: 采用微磁模拟方法，系统研究纳米螺旋的几何参数：曲率、扭转（ω）、偏心度（ε）、膜厚度和内径，分析这些参数对剩磁态和矫顽场的影响。

Result: 发现双稳态磁行为，磁化反转过程中出现涡旋-畴壁传播；识别出四种简并的混合剩磁态构型；当长轴（较大内径）比短轴大约30%时，矫顽场随偏心度增加而增加，但对扭转变化不敏感。

Conclusion: 纳米螺旋在系统几何变形下表现出鲁棒的双稳态和增强的矫顽性，这些特性源于几何诱导的膜表面磁静电荷修饰，显示出在三维纳米磁学应用中的潜力。

Abstract: We investigate the equilibrium magnetization in ferromagnetic nanoscrews (NSw) using micromagnetic simulations. These systems consist of elongated three-dimensional magnetic membranes with helicoidal geometry, combining curvature, torsion ($\mathrm{w}$), and eccentricity ($ε$) along their length. We focus on the influence of these geometric parameters, together with membrane thickness and inner diameter, on remanent states and coercive fields. Our results, obtained over a broad range of eccentricities and torsions, reveal bistable magnetic behavior, with vortex-domain-wall propagation during magnetization reversal. We identify four degenerate configurations of a remarkably stable mixed remanent state. The coercive field is found to increase with eccentricity for structures with a major axis (larger inner diameter) approximately 30\% larger than the minor axis (smaller inner diameter), while remaining largely insensitive to variations in torsion. These findings are interpreted in terms of geometry-induced modifications of surface magnetostatic charges on the membrane mantle. Overall, our results demonstrate that nanoscrews exhibit robust bistability under systematic geometric deformation, together with enhanced coercivity, highlighting their potential for applications in three-dimensional nanomagnetism.

</details>


### [101] [Suppression of Superconductivity and Electrostatic Side Gate Tuning in High Mobility SrTiO$_3$ Surface Electron Gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22205)
*Dickson Boahen,Sushant Padhye,Gayan De Silva,Eshanvi Rao,Evgeny Mikheev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在SrTiO₃衬底上通过氢等离子体曝光制备图案化高迁移率二维电子气，迁移率高达7400 cm²/V·s，但未观察到超导转变，表明高迁移率准二维SrTiO₃电子气中超导被抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在SrTiO₃衬底上制备高迁移率二维电子气(2DEG)并研究其超导特性，同时研究静电栅极调制和量子器件图案化的可能性。

Method: 采用氢等离子体曝光技术在SrTiO₃衬底表面制备图案化高迁移率二维电子气，通过控制样品老化系统探索电子密度范围，在约10 mK的低温下测量超导特性，并研究静电栅极调制及其与电子密度和侧栅几何结构的标度关系。

Result: 器件迁移率高达7400 cm²/V·s，在包括STO 2DEG超导圆顶预期范围的宽电子密度范围内均未观察到超导转变；静电栅极调制能力随侧栅与沟道间距增大而改善；低电子密度下观察到随机沟道夹断事件，形成具有不规则电导量子化的准弹道收缩。

Conclusion: 高迁移率准二维SrTiO₃电子气中的超导被抑制，可能与垂直限制和电子轨道重排有关；这种无需外延的高迁移率氧化物材料平台为图案化量子器件提供了新途径。

Abstract: We report on the fabrication and characterization of patterned high-mobility two-dimensional electron gases (2DEG) formed on SrTiO$_3$ (STO) substrate surfaces by hydrogen plasma exposure. The resulting devices consistently showed high electron mobilities up to 7400 cm$^2$/V$\cdot$s. A large range of electron density was systematically explored by controlled aging of the sample between cooldowns, including the expected range for the STO 2DEG superconducting dome. No superconducting transition was observed down to the base temperature of approximately 10 mK. This suggests suppression of superconductivity in high mobility quasi-two-dimensional SrTiO$_3$ electron gas, likely linked to vertical confinement and electronic orbital rearrangement. We systematically explored electrostatic gate modulation in this 2DEG system and its scaling with electron density and side gate geometry. In contrast with our initial expectation, we observed an improvement of achievable total modulation for larger side gate to channel separation. At low electron density, stochastic channel pinch-off events were observed, creating quasi-ballistic constrictions with irregular conductance quantization. This epitaxy-free and high mobility oxide material platform offers a promising new route towards patterning quantum devices.

</details>


### [102] [Semiclassical Wave-Packet Dynamics in Phase-Space Geometry: Quantum Metric Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21262)
*Luca Maranzana,Koki Shinada,Ying-Ming Xie,Sergey Artyukhin,Naoto Nagaosa*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个基于ħ展开的量子几何统一形式体系，将实空间和动量空间几何平等对待，推导了量子度规对波包能量、贝里联络和相空间态密度的修正，并建立了包含量子度规效应的全相空间动力学方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何在量子材料的输运和光学现象中起关键作用。近期研究通过量子几何张量探索了电磁学和引力的类比，其实部和虚部分别对应量子度规和贝里曲率。然而，需要建立一个统一的形式体系来同时处理实空间和动量空间的量子几何效应。

Method: 采用基于ħ展开的通用形式体系，这等价于空间导数展开。将实空间和动量空间几何平等对待，推导了量子度规对波包能量、贝里联络和相空间态密度的修正。建立了包含量子度规效应的全相空间动力学方程，并识别了度规梯度诱导的极化和混合分量产生的线性霍尔响应。

Result: 获得了量子度规对波包能量、贝里联络和相空间态密度的修正，类似于非线性响应中的场诱导修正。建立了捕获全相空间量子度规效应的动力学方程。发现了度规梯度诱导的极化和混合分量产生的线性霍尔响应。

Conclusion: 该框架为研究实空间和动量空间量子几何共存系统中的热力学和输运性质提供了基础，统一处理了量子度规效应，扩展了量子几何在材料科学中的应用范围。

Abstract: Quantum geometry governs a wide range of transport and optical phenomena in quantum materials. Recent works have explored analogue electromagnetism and gravity in terms of the quantum geometric tensor, whose real and imaginary parts correspond to the quantum metric and the Berry curvature. By treating real- and momentum-space geometries on an equal footing, we develop a comprehensive and general formalism based on an expansion in $\hbar$, equivalent to an expansion in spatial derivatives. We derive the quantum-metric corrections to the wave-packet energy, the Berry connection, and the phase-space density of states, similar to the field-induced corrections in nonlinear response. A kinetic equation that captures quantum-metric effects across the full phase space then follows naturally. We further identify a polarization induced by gradients of the metric and a linear Hall response originating from its mixed components. Our framework provides a foundation for investigating thermodynamic and transport properties in systems where real- and momentum-space quantum geometries coexist.

</details>


### [103] [Joule heating and electronic Gurzhi effect in hydrodynamic differential transport in an electron liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21346)
*Yi Wang,Shu-Yu Zheng,Li Lu,Kai Chang,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在GaAs/AlGaAs量子阱中研究电子液体的流体动力学输运，发现在零磁场下U型交流电流回路驱动的非线性输运呈现洛伦兹线型，直流偏置电流会加深谷值，该现象归因于焦耳热对电子温度的加热效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究电子液体在流体动力学区域中的输运特性，特别是探索直流偏置电流对非线性输运的影响机制，理解电子温度变化对粘度电阻的调控作用。

Method: 在GaAs/AlGaAs量子阱中进行微分电阻测量，采用U型交流电流回路驱动非线性输运，结合交流+直流测量技术，通过施加外部直流电流偏置，分析电子温度变化对输运特性的影响。

Result: 零磁场下非线性输运呈现洛伦兹线型；直流偏置电流加深输运谷值；分析表明该现象由焦耳热导致的电子温度升高引起；定量证明粘度电阻与温度平方成反比关系，与不同测量配置下的直流电流诱导电子Gurzhi效应一致。

Conclusion: 观察到的直流电流诱导电子输运谷值归因于焦耳热对电子温度的加热效应，粘度电阻的温度依赖性支持电子液体中的Gurzhi效应，为理解流体动力学电子输运中的热效应提供了实验证据。

Abstract: We perform a differential resistance study in the hydrodynamic regime of electron liquid in GaAs/AlGaAs quantum wells. At zero magnetic field ($B$) a Lorentzian profile occurs in the nonlinear transport driven by a U-turn (ac) current loop, in (ac + dc) measurements a minimum deepens with the external dc current bias ($j_{dc}$). Our analysis shows that the observed electronic transport valley induced by $j_{dc}$ is attributed to Joule heating effect on the electron temperature ($T_{e}$) of electron liquid. Quantitatively, we demonstrate that the viscosity resistivity ($Δρ$) is proportional to $T^{-2}$ and is consistent with the dc-current induced electronic Gurzhi effect in various configurations of measurement.

</details>


### [104] [Observation of microscopic domain effects in the metal-insulator transition of thin-film NdNiO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21405)
*Lucy S. Nathwani,Anne Ruperto,Ashvini Vallipuram,Abigail Y. Jiang,Grace A. Pan,Dan Ferenc Segedin,Ari B. Turkiewicz,Charles M. Brooks,Jarad A. Mason,Qichen Song,Julia A. Mundy*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究使用频域热反射和频域光反射技术测量了NdNiO₃薄膜在金属-绝缘体相变中的热导率和载流子扩散率变化，揭示了面外热输运与电荷输运的无滞后特性，并归因于纳米尺度畴的各向异性渗流效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙钛矿氧化物薄膜在相变过程中展现出可调的电学、磁学和热学性质，但传统热输运测量技术受基底主导，难以准确表征薄膜的热输运特性。研究旨在开发敏感探测量子材料相变的新方法，并探索NdNiO₃在热控制和存储器应用中的潜力。

Method: 采用频域热反射(FDTR)测量外延NdNiO₃薄膜的面外热导率，同时使用频域光反射(FDPR)测量光激发载流子的双极扩散率变化。通过对比面内电阻的滞后行为与面外热输运和电荷输运的特性，分析相变过程中的各向异性输运机制。

Result: FDTR测量显示NdNiO₃薄膜在金属-绝缘体相变过程中面外热导率发生急剧变化。FDPR测量揭示光激发载流子的双极扩散率显著改变。虽然面内电阻表现出大的滞后现象，但面外热输运和电荷输运几乎无滞后。这种差异归因于当薄膜厚度接近畴长度尺度时，纳米尺度畴在相变过程中各向异性渗流效应。

Conclusion: FDTR和FDPR技术被确立为探测量子材料相变的灵敏工具。NdNiO₃薄膜在相变中表现出的各向异性输运特性使其在热控制和存储器应用中具有重要潜力，特别是面外热输运的无滞后特性为热管理器件设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Perovskite oxides display correlated electrical, magnetic, and thermal properties that can be further tuned in the thin-film limit, making them contenders for next-generation electronics. Measuring thermal transport in thin films is challenging, because traditional techniques are dominated by the substrate. Here, frequency-domain thermoreflectance (FDTR) of an epitaxial NdNiO$_3$ thin film reveals a sharp change in out-of-plane thermal conductivity across the metal-insulator transition. Complementary frequency-domain photoreflectance (FDPR) reveals a large change in ambipolar diffusivity of photoexcited carriers. While the in-plane electrical resistance shows large hysteresis, out-of-plane thermal and charge transport shows negligible hysteresis. We attribute this discrepancy to anisotropy in the percolation of nanoscale domains across the transition as the film thickness approaches the domain length scale. We establish FDTR and FDPR as sensitive probes of quantum material phase transitions and highlight NdNiO$_3$ for thermal control and memory applications.

</details>


### [105] [A closed-loop platform for the design and nanoscale imaging of GHz acoustic metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21744)
*Federico Maccagno,Jasleen Kaur,Benjamin H. November,Layan Ansari,Daria-Teodora Harabor,Rares-Georgian Mihalcea,Harris Pirie,Jennifer E. Hoffman*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究开发了静电力显微镜技术，实现了对表面声波超材料中GHz频段亚微米尺度行波的实时空间成像，成功绘制了完整频段的复杂能带结构，包括狄拉克锥和声子输运机制转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面声波超材料的能带工程对经典通信和量子信息处理有重要意义，但现有成像技术无法在GHz宽频带内解析亚微米尺度的行波，只能捕获离散频率的片段信息，限制了系统表征和控制能力。

Method: 开发静电力显微镜技术，利用亚200纳米空间分辨率、宽GHz带宽和非接触成像，在LiNbO₃上的蜂窝超材料中实现行波的实时空间成像，能够连续频率分辨并保持亚晶格细节。

Result: 成功绘制了SAW石墨烯类似物狄拉克点周围完整频段的能带结构，包括声学狄拉克锥，观察到从弹道到扩散输运机制的转变；通过打破亚晶格对称性调控狄拉克点带隙开口，并成像了频率依赖的波局域化。

Conclusion: 该EFM技术填补了设计与实空间验证之间的空白，为下一代通信、微流控和量子声学应用中任意SAW景观的工程设计提供了高效工具，实现了系统性的能带工程控制。

Abstract: Band structure engineering in surface acoustic wave (SAW) metamaterials could advance both classical telecommunications and quantum information processing. However, no imaging technique has demonstrated the necessary capability to resolve sub-$μ$m traveling SAWs across wide GHz bandwidths. Existing methods capture only fragments of the dispersion at discrete frequencies, preventing systematic characterization and control of SAW-based metamaterials. Here, we develop electrostatic force microscopy (EFM) to enable real-space imaging of traveling SAWs in honeycomb metamaterials on LiNbO$_3$. Our application leverages sub-200 nm spatial resolution, broad GHz bandwidth, and non-contact imaging to map complex band structures with continuous frequency resolution and expanded frequency range, while preserving sub-lattice detail. Using EFM, we map the full relevant frequency range around the Dirac point of a SAW graphene analog, including the acoustic Dirac cones, and the transition from ballistic to diffusive SAW transport regime. Furthermore, by breaking sublattice symmetry, we tune the opening of a band gap at the Dirac point, and image frequency-dependent wave localization on sublattice sites. Our EFM technique closes the loop between design and real-space validation, streamlining the engineering of arbitrary SAW landscapes for next-generation applications spanning telecommunications, microfluidics, and quantum acoustics.

</details>


### [106] [Antiferromagnetic Pure Spin Current Memdevices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21755)
*Martin Latorre,Gaspar De la Barrera,Roberto E. Troncoso,Alvaro S. Nunez*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出一种基于反铁磁组件的新型纯自旋电流类存储器件的理论基础——自旋磁阻抗效应，利用自旋-赖斯-梅勒哈密顿量和磁场梯度打破反演对称性来生成和控制自旋电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压电自旋电子效应等机制表明应变或晶格畸变可诱导偶极自旋矩变化产生纯自旋电流，这为低功耗信息处理和新型器件架构开辟了新可能。本文旨在探索基于反铁磁组件的纯自旋电流存储器件的理论基础。

Method: 提出"自旋磁阻抗效应"作为理论框架，聚焦于可用自旋-赖斯-梅勒哈密顿量建模的材料，引入明确打破反演对称性的磁场梯度，研究自旋电流的生成和控制机制。

Result: 研究结果阐明了自旋电流如何在这些材料中生成和控制，为理解自旋-赖斯-梅勒系统在自旋电子学应用中的潜力提供了新见解。

Conclusion: 提出的自旋磁阻抗效应为基于反铁磁组件的纯自旋电流类存储器件奠定了理论基础，展示了这些材料在下一代自旋电子技术中的潜力，特别是在低功耗信息处理方面。

Abstract: Spin currents can be generated through various mechanisms, including the piezospintronic effect, which arises when strain or lattice distortions induce a change in the dipolar spin moment, causing a pure spin current without necessarily being accompanied by net charge transport. This opens new possibilities for low-power information processing and novel device architectures. In this work, we propose a novel effect, the spintronic-magneto-impedictive effect, as the theoretical basis for a pure spin-current memory-like device based on antiferromagnetic components. We focus on materials that can be modeled by the so-called spin-Rice-Mele Hamiltonian, incorporating a magnetic field gradient that explicitly breaks inversion symmetry. Our results shed light on how spin currents are generated and controlled, providing new insights into the potential of these materials for next-generation spintronic technologies.

</details>


### [107] [Invariant ionic conductance in an atomically thin polar nanopore](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21827)
*Shengping Zhang,Haiou Zeng,Ningran Wu,Guodong Xue,Xiao Li,Anshul Saxena,Junhe Tong,Nianjie Liang,Ying Wang,Zeyu Zhuang,Jing Yang,Narayana R. Aluru,Kaihui Liu,Bai Song,Luda Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究利用单层MoSSe纳米孔实现了跨越六个数量级盐浓度下不变的离子电导，模拟了细胞膜中偶极电位的功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿生物细胞膜中的偶极电位一直是人工离子通道领域的重大挑战，因为偶极电位具有埃米级的特征长度。本研究旨在探索具有本征偶极和原子厚度的单层MoSSe纳米孔来解决这一问题。

Method: 使用单层二硫化钼硒（MoSSe）纳米孔，利用其本征偶极和原子厚度特性，通过分子动力学模拟研究偶极调控的纳米受限水的介电性质对离子传输的影响。

Result: 在跨越六个数量级的盐浓度范围内观察到了不变的离子电导，这与所有已知的电导-浓度标度律都不同，类似于高浓度下细胞膜中的电流饱和现象。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了一种奇异的电导标度律，为以前所未有的方式控制离子传输开辟了新途径，并突出了偶极调控的纳米受限水介电性质在离子传输中的基本作用。

Abstract: Ion channels regulate many essential properties of biological cells, especially the membrane potential. Despite decades of efforts on artificial channels, it remains a great challenge to mimic the dipole potential-an indispensable constituent of the membrane potential, due to its angstrom-scale characteristic length. Here, we explore nanopores in monolayer molybdenum sulfide selenide (MoSSe) considering its intrinsic dipole and atomic thickness. Remarkably, an invariant ionic conductance was observed over salt concentrations spanning six orders of magnitude, distinct from all known conductance-concentration scaling laws and reminiscent of the current saturation in cell membranes at high concentrations. Molecular dynamics simulations revealed the fundamental role of the dipole-modulated dielectric properties of nanoconfined water. Our findings highlight an exotic conductance scaling law and open up a novel avenue for controlling ion transport in unprecedented ways.

</details>


### [108] [Efficient photo-Nernst terahertz emission in single heavy-metal films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21924)
*Lei Wang,Linxuan Song,Elbert E. M. Chia,Peijie Sun,Jianlin Luo,Rongyan Chen,Yong-Chang Lau,Xinbo Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在低温磁场条件下，独立铂纳米薄膜通过超快光能斯特效应产生高效太赫兹辐射，重新定义了重金属在太赫兹发射中的角色


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的太赫兹发射器主要依赖自旋电子异质结构，其中重金属仅作为被动的自旋-电荷转换器。本研究旨在探索重金属作为主动太赫兹发射器的潜力，重新定义其在太赫兹发射中的角色。

Method: 研究使用独立的铂纳米薄膜在低温条件下施加外部磁场，通过超快光能斯特效应产生太赫兹辐射。通过对比铂、钨和钽的发射极性验证机制，并采用厚度缩放和合金化抑制热导率的方法增强发射效率。

Result: 实验证实了超快光能斯特效应是主导机制，太赫兹发射极性直接由能斯特系数符号决定。厚度缩放和合金化抑制热导率均能显著增强单层发射效率，使其达到与基准自旋电子双层结构相当的水平。

Conclusion: 该研究将重金属从被动的自旋汇重新定义为主动的太赫兹发射器，揭示了一种适用于多种自旋电子和量子材料的通用发射范式，为太赫兹发射技术提供了新的设计思路。

Abstract: State-of-the-art metallic terahertz (THz) emitters rely predominantly on spintronic heterostructures, where heavy metals serve as passive spin-to-charge converters. Here, we demonstrate efficient THz radiation from standalone Pt nanofilms at cryogenic temperatures and under external magnetic fields. The governing mechanism is identified as the ultrafast photo-Nernst effect, wherein a transient thermal gradient drives a transverse charge current. The THz emission polarity is directly dictated by the sign of the Nernst coefficient, as verified by the phase reversal observed between Pt and W or Ta. Remarkably, both thickness scaling and alloying-induced suppression of thermal conductivity independently amplify the single-layer emission to levels comparable with benchmark spintronic bilayers. These findings redefine the established role of heavy metals from passive spin-sinks to active THz emitters, uncovering a universal emission paradigm applicable across diverse spintronic and quantum materials.

</details>


### [109] [Floquet generation of hybrid-order topology and $\mathbb{Z}_2$-like bipolar localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21954)
*Koustav Roy,Latu Kalita,B. Tanatar,Saurabh Basu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文研究了通过周期驱动和非互易耦合工程化的高阶拓扑绝缘体，展示了如何动态生成和控制静态系统中隐藏的厄米与非厄米拓扑特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索如何通过动态调控（周期驱动和非互易耦合）来激活和控制在静态BBH模型中隐藏的拓扑特征，包括一阶拓扑边缘态、高阶角态以及非厄米趋肤效应。

Method: 采用Benalcazar-Bernevig-Hughes (BBH)模型作为基础，通过两阶段工程化：首先施加周期驱动（Floquet调制），然后引入非互易耦合。利用广义布里渊区理论分析非厄米趋肤效应，并通过镜像对称性构建对应的1D映射来简化2D分析。

Result: 1. 周期驱动激活了一阶拓扑，产生了Floquet相，在零和π准能量处同时存在一阶导电边缘态和高阶角态，形成了混合阶拓扑相。2. 非互易耦合产生了Z2类趋肤效应，展示了从单极到双极局域化相的驱动诱导转变。3. 发现了完全抑制趋肤效应的条件。4. 成功构建了对应的1D映射来简化2D系统的分析。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了一种高效的技术，能够动态生成和控制厄米与非厄米拓扑特征，这些特征在静态场景中是被掩盖的。通过周期驱动和非互易耦合的工程化，实现了对拓扑相（包括混合阶拓扑相和趋肤效应）的主动调控，为拓扑物态的动态操控提供了新途径。

Abstract: Higher order topology, in the form of the emergence of corner modes, is observed in two dimensions when crystalline symmetries are superposed on the Altland-Zirnbauer classification of topological insulators. It occurs in Benalcazar-Bernevig-Hughes (BBH) model on a 2D square lattice, which owing to an embedded $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge field, features a bulk quadrupole moment with localized zero-energy corner states. Further, as a dividend, the BBH model transmutes the general notion of the space-time inversion ($\mathcal{PT}$) symmetry and behaves as a spinful system, without having to invoke `real' spin degrees of freedom. A two-fold engineering of the model, namely a periodic drive, followed by a non-reciprocal hopping render intriguing consequences. As a first, the drive activates first-order topology, and the resulting Floquet phase hosts a coexistence of first-order conducting edges at both zero and $π$ quasienergies along with higher order corner states, which qualifies the coexisting state to be denoted as a hybrid-order topological phase. Further, inclusion of non-reciprocal couplings features a $\mathbb{Z}_2$-like skin effect which demonstrates a drive-induced transition from a unipolar to a bipolar localized phase, and is evidenced via the generalized Brillouin zone (GBZ) theory. While depiction of a GBZ in 2D is challenging, a corresponding 1D map is still possible and can be implemented by exploiting the mirror symmetry. We further uncover conditions under which the skin effect is completely suppressed in our system. Putting together, our results manifest an efficient technique to dynamically engender and control Hermitian and non-Hermitian topological features, that remain otherwise masked in a static scenario.

</details>


### [110] [Engineering chiral-induced spin selectivity in an artificial topological quantum well](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21964)
*Lizhou Liu,Peng-Yi Liu,Tian-Yi Zhang,Qing-Feng Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在InAs/GaSb量子阱中通过工程化手性结构和退相干电极实现了手性诱导自旋选择性效应，证明了固态平台中可控产生CISS效应的可行性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受手性诱导自旋选择性(CISS)现象启发，希望在固态系统中实现可控的CISS效应。CISS现象中非自旋极化的电子通过手性介质后变为自旋极化，理论研究表明自旋轨道耦合、几何手性和退相干共同作用产生该效应

Method: 在工程化的InAs/GaSb量子阱中引入可控的几何手性和退相干。通过引入手性结构并翻转手性来观察自旋极化符号变化，同时系统性地增加退相干电极数量来研究极化幅度变化，并与非手性配置进行对比

Result: 手性结构产生清晰的自旋极化，其符号在手性翻转时反转，幅度随退相干电极数量系统性地增加。非手性配置无自旋选择性。极化在强安德森无序下保持稳定，表明工程化手性结构提供了内在稳定的自旋选择性传输途径

Conclusion: 在拓扑量子阱系统中建立了固态平台，能够可控地产生CISS效应，为固态自旋电子学应用提供了新途径

Abstract: Chiral-induced spin selectivity (CISS) is a striking phenomenon in which spin-unpolarized electrons become spin-polarized after traversing a chiral medium. Theoretical studies have shown that spin-orbit coupling, geometric chirality, and dephasing act cooperatively for this effect to emerge. Inspired by this, we demonstrate a solid-state realization of CISS in an engineered InAs/GaSb quantum well where geometric chirality and dephasing can be introduced controllably. Introducing a chiral structure produces a clear spin polarization whose sign reverses when the chirality is flipped, and whose magnitude grows systematically with the number of dephasing electrodes, while achiral configurations exhibit no spin selectivity. The polarization remains robust even under strong Anderson disorder, showing that the engineered chiral structures provides an intrinsically stable route to spin-selective transport. These results establish a solid-state platform in the topological quantum well system for controllably generating the CISS effect.

</details>


### [111] [Electrically controllable valence-conduction band reversals in helical trilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21973)
*Matan Bocarsly,Indranil Roy,Weifeng Zhi,Li-Qiao Xia,Aviram Uri,Yves H. Kwan,Aaron Sharpe,Matan Uzan,Yuri Myasoedov,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Trithep Devakul,Pablo Jarillo-Herrero,Eli Zeldov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 螺旋三层石墨烯中发现了一种新的相互作用驱动机制，通过掺杂控制实现了自旋、谷和子晶格三个内部自由度的循环反转相变


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究莫尔石墨烯系统中电子相互作用如何打破对称性，特别是在螺旋三层石墨烯中探索新的相互作用驱动机制

Method: 使用扫描纳米SQUID磁力计进行磁测量，结合自洽Hartree-Fock计算分析八个低能平带的相互作用重组

Result: 检测到一系列尖锐的磁信号，表现为跷跷板式转变，价带和导带在掺杂过程中反复互换，伴随新型磁滞现象；计算显示相互作用驱动轨道磁化急剧变化

Conclusion: HTG是首个通过电子相互作用提供掺杂控制访问所有三个内部自由度（自旋、谷、子晶格）的系统，引入了一类新的关联相变

Abstract: In moiré graphene systems, electronic interactions lift spin and valley degeneracies, leading to symmetry-broken ground states. In helical trilayer graphene (HTG), we uncover a distinct interaction-driven mechanism in which the roles of sublattice-polarized valence and conduction bands are cyclically reversed. Using scanning nano-SQUID magnetometry, we detect a series of sharp magnetic signatures consistent with seesaw-like transitions, where occupied and unoccupied valence and conduction bands interchange repeatedly with doping, accompanied by a novel form of magnetic hysteresis. These transitions occur entirely within metallic regimes and leave only weak fingerprints in transport measurements. Self-consistent Hartree-Fock calculations reveal that interactions reorganize all eight low-energy flat bands, driving abrupt changes in orbital magnetization. Our results establish HTG as the first system where electronic interactions provide doping-controlled access to all three internal degrees of freedom - spin, valley, and sublattice - introducing a new class of correlated phase transitions.

</details>


### [112] [Spin Elasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21981)
*Zhong-Chen Gao,Tianyi Zhang,Feifei Wang,Jingguo Hu,Peng Yan,Xiufeng Han*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文发现自旋自由度中存在弹性特性，提出了"自旋弹性"概念，揭示了弹性在物质和自旋空间中的普遍性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上认为弹性是物质介质独有的特性，但作者发现自旋自由度中可能隐藏着类似的弹性机制，这促使他们探索自旋形态的可恢复变形特性。

Method: 引入"自旋弹性"概念作为内在机制，用于描述自旋形态的可恢复变形。通过建立自旋对应物，将弹性概念从物质空间扩展到自旋空间。

Result: 揭示了弹性在物质和自旋空间中的普遍性，表明弹性是维持物理领域结构完整性的基础机制。发现了自旋形态的可恢复变形特性。

Conclusion: 这项工作通过建立缺失的自旋对应物，完善了弹性理论框架，并指向一个更广泛的范式：弹性超越了传统边界，在物质和自旋空间中都具有普遍性。

Abstract: Elasticity has long been regarded as a property exclusive to material media. Here we uncover its hidden existence in the spin degree of freedom. We introduce spin elasticity-an intrinsic mechanism that governs recoverable deformation of spin morphology. This discovery reveals a previously unrecognized universality: elasticity operates in both matter and spin spaces, underpinning structural integrity across physical realms. By establishing the missing spin counterpart, this work completes the elastic picture and points toward a broader paradigm where elasticity transcends its conventional boundaries.

</details>


### [113] [Nonlinear suppression of dispersion broadening of ultrashort spin-wave pulses in thin YIG films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22051)
*K. O. Nikolaev,D. Raskhodchikov,J. Bensmann,I. V. Borisenko,E. Lomonte,L. Jin,R. Schmidt,J. Kern,S. Michaelis de Vasconcellos,R. Bratschitsch,S. O. Demokritov,W. H. P. Pernice,V. E. Demidov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在纳米厚YIG薄膜中，前向体自旋波短脉冲的非线性传播可实现包络孤子形成，抵消色散展宽


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究微观自旋波电路中色散效应对信息传输的不利影响，探索通过非线性效应抵消色散展宽的方法

Method: 实验研究纳米厚YIG薄膜中前向体自旋波短脉冲的非线性传播，分析非线性与色散的相互作用

Result: 在约1毫瓦微波功率下达到孤子形成阈值，3纳秒自旋波脉冲可在50微米距离内传输而无时间展宽

Conclusion: 非线性自旋系统可有效抵消色散展宽，形成包络孤子，为微观自旋波电路中的高速信息传输提供有前景的途径

Abstract: We study experimentally the nonlinear propagation of short pulses of forward volume spin waves in nanometer-thick YIG films. We show that nonlinearity of the spin system can efficiently counteract dispersion broadening of the pulses, leading to the formation of envelope solitons. We demonstrate that in microscopic YIG systems, microwave powers of the order of one milliwatt are sufficient to reach the soliton formation threshold. At powers slightly above this threshold, we achieve transmission of 3-ns spin-wave pulses over distances of up to 50 micrometers without increase in their temporal width. Our results demonstrate a promising way towards high-rate transmission of information in microscopic spin-wave circuits unaffected by detrimental dispersion effects.

</details>


### [114] [Landau-Level-Resolved Mode Mixing and Shot Noise in Gate-Defined Graphene Quantum Point Contacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22111)
*Shakthidhar Vilvanathan,Jerin Saji,Kristiana Frei,Jakub Tworzydlo,Manohar Kumar*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 石墨烯量子点接触在量子霍尔区表现出三种传输机制，通过散粒噪声和法诺因子可区分单通道与多通道混沌输运，其中零朗道能级产生F=1/3的独特特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯量子点接触在量子霍尔区存在多种竞争传输机制（手性边缘传播、谷简并、栅极诱导模式混合），这些机制在电导测量中无法区分，需要散粒噪声来探测传输本征值的统计特性。

Method: 开发混合框架：结合紧束缚模拟栅极定义的石墨烯量子点接触与随机矩阵理论，预测散粒噪声和法诺因子特征，并用hBN封装石墨烯霍尔棒实验电导图进行验证。

Result: 识别三种不同机制：绝热传播、尖锐模式滤波、栅极下局域态驱动的多模式混合。高朗道能级(N_L>0)完全模式混合产生通用混沌腔极限F≈1/4；零朗道能级(N_L=0)收敛到F=1/3，源于其边缘态的子晶格极化特性。

Conclusion: F=1/3与F=1/4的交叉是朗道能级分辨的噪声特征，为石墨烯量子点接触中单通道与多通道混沌输运提供了直接区分标志，这一特征在电导测量中无法获得。

Abstract: Graphene quantum point contacts (QPCs) in the quantum Hall regime host competing transport mechanisms including chiral edge propagation, valley degeneracy, and gate-induced mode mixing. Their interplay is not visible in conductance alone. Shot noise directly probes the statistics of transmission eigenvalues, revealing microscopic mode partitioning that conductance cannot access. We develop a hybrid framework combining tight-binding simulations of gate-defined graphene QPCs with random matrix theory (RMT) to predict shot noise and Fano factor signatures across different quantum Hall regimes, validated against experimental conductance maps of hBN-encapsulated graphene Hall bars. Three distinct regimes are identified: adiabatic propagation, sharp mode filtering, and multi-mode mixing driven by localized states beneath the split gate. For higher Landau levels ($N_L > 0$), complete mode mixing produces the universal chaotic-cavity limit $F \simeq 1/4$. Strikingly, the zeroth Landau level ($N_L = 0$) converges to $F = 1/3$. This distinct value originates in the sublattice polarization of the $N_L = 0$ edge state: coupling to mixed-sublattice localized states beneath the gate is suppressed, confining transport to an effective single channel ($N = 1$). Complete mixing within this single channel yields a flat transmission eigenvalue distribution and hence exactly $F = 1/3$ from single-channel RMT, numerically coincident with but mechanistically distinct from pseudo-diffusive zero-field graphene transport. The $F = 1/3$ versus $F = 1/4$ crossover is a Landau-level-resolved noise signature absent in conductance, providing a direct discriminator between single-channel and multi-channel chaotic transport in graphene QPCs.

</details>


### [115] [Supercurrent-Driven Néel Torque in Superconductor/Altermagnet Hybrids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22243)
*Hamed Vakili,Moaz Ali,Igor Žutić,Alexey A. Kovalev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在超导体/反铁磁异质结中预测了超流驱动的奈尔自旋轨道力矩，与自旋三重态关联的出现相关，可用于无耗散控制奈尔矢量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超导体与反铁磁材料异质结中的新型自旋轨道力矩效应，实现无耗散的自旋电子学控制，为赛道存储器、无耗散超导电子学和非常规计算提供新平台。

Method: 理论预测超导体/反铁磁异质结中的超流驱动奈尔自旋轨道力矩，分析超流诱导的自旋极化机制，研究自旋轨道耦合与动量依赖自旋分裂的相互作用，探讨奈尔矢量方向对超流的调控作用。

Result: 预测了超流驱动的奈尔自旋轨道力矩，该效应源于自旋三重态关联的出现；超流可通过奈尔矢量方向调控；该力矩既能推动磁畴壁，也能在畴壁内反转奈尔矢量方向。

Conclusion: 超导体/反铁磁异质结为奈尔矢量的无耗散控制提供了多功能平台，在赛道存储器、无耗散超导电子学和非常规计算中具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: We predict a supercurrent-driven Néel spin-orbit torque in a superconductor/$d$-wave altermagnet heterostructure, associated with the emergence of spin-triplet correlations. The effect can be understood as a consequence of the supercurrent-induced spin polarization, owing to the interplay between spin-orbit coupling and momentum-dependent spin splitting, as found, for example, in altermagnets. Remarkably, the supercurrent can be tuned by the Néel-vector direction, and the supercurrent-induced torque can both propel magnetic domain walls and reverse the Néel-vector orientation within a domain wall. These findings establish superconductor/altermagnet heterostructures as a versatile platform for the dissipationless control of the Néel vector, with potential applications in racetrack memory, dissipationless superconducting electronics, and unconventional computing.

</details>
