### [134] [A chemical language model for reticular materials design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20389)
*Dhruv Menon,Vivek Singh,Xu Chen,Mohammad Reza Alizadeh Kiapi,Ivan Zyuzin,Hamish W. Macleod,Nakul Rampal,William Shepard,Omar M. Yaghi,David Fairen-Jimenez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: Nexerra-R1是一个用于网状化学逆设计的构建块化学语言模型，通过靶向生成有机连接体实现从计算设计到可合成框架的直接转化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网状化学已合成数万种金属有机框架，但新材料发现仍主要依赖直觉驱动的连接体设计和迭代实验，仅探索了网状材料巨大化学空间的一小部分，限制了具有目标性质框架的系统性发现。

Method: 开发Nexerra-R1构建块化学语言模型，在分子构建块层面操作，支持低连接性连接体的无约束生成和与预定义节点/拓扑兼容的对称多齿基序的支架约束设计，结合流引导分布靶向引导生成过程朝向应用相关目标。

Result: 模型成功重新发现了已知MOFs，并提出了完全在计算机中生成的先前未报道框架CU-525的实验合成方案，验证了从计算设计到可合成框架的直接转化策略。

Conclusion: 建立了一个通用的网状材料逆设计范式，其中可控化学语言建模实现了从计算设计到可合成框架的直接转化，为网状化学的系统性材料发现提供了新途径。

Abstract: Reticular chemistry has enabled the synthesis of tens of thousands of metal-organic frameworks (MOFs), yet the discovery of new materials still relies largely on intuition-driven linker design and iterative experimentation. As a result, researchers explore only a small fraction of the vast chemical space accessible to reticular materials, limiting the systematic discovery of frameworks with targeted properties. Here, we introduce Nexerra-R1, a building-block chemical language model that enables inverse design in reticular chemistry through the targeted generation of organic linkers. Rather than generating complete frameworks directly, Nexerra-R1 operates at the level of molecular building blocks, preserving the modular logic that underpins reticular synthesis. The model supports both unconstrained generation of low-connectivity linkers and scaffold-constrained design of symmetric multidentate motifs compatible with predefined nodes and topologies. We further combine linker generation with flow-guided distributional targeting to steer the generative process toward application-relevant objectives while maintaining chemical validity and assembly feasibility. The generated linkers are subsequently assembled into three-dimensional frameworks and are structurally optimized to produce candidate materials compatible with experimental synthesis. Using Nexerra-R1, we validate this strategy by rediscovering known MOFs and by proposing the experimental synthesis of a previously unreported framework, CU-525, generated entirely in silico. Together, these results establish a general inverse-design paradigm for reticular materials in which controllable chemical language modelling enables the direct translation from computational design to synthesizable frameworks.

</details>


### [135] [Structural Phase Separation Couples to Charge-Density-Wave Formation in Kagome Metal FeGe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20459)
*Boyang Zhao,Youngjun Ahn,Qinwen Deng,Yidai Liu,Sijie Xu,Donald A. Walko,Stephan O. Hruszkewycz,Pengcheng Dai,Liang Wu,Haidan Wen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: FeGe中电荷密度波(CDW)相变是一阶结构相变，伴随晶格常数变化，晶格-电荷强耦合对稳定长程CDW序起关键作用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究kagome金属FeGe中电荷、自旋和晶格自由度交织如何影响其CDW和反铁磁有序的涌现性质，特别是结构相变在CDW形成中的关键作用

Method: 使用温度依赖的高分辨率X射线衍射测量，观测晶格反射峰的分裂；进行朗道自由能分析研究晶格-电荷耦合

Result: 在CDW转变温度T_CDW处观察到晶格反射分裂为两个共存峰，对应不同晶格常数，证明存在一阶结构相变；长程CDW序仅与压缩的面外晶格常数结构相称

Conclusion: 强晶格-电荷耦合是稳定长程CDW序的关键因素，结构转变在CDW形成中起决定性作用，为FeGe中电子相的应变控制提供了机会

Abstract: The intertwining of charge, spin, and lattice degrees of freedom underlies the emergent properties of correlated materials. A recent prominent example is the kagome metal FeGe, which hosts coexisting charge density wave (CDW) and antiferromagnetic orders, accompanied by a lattice distortion associated with partial Ge-Ge dimerization. Using temperature-dependent high-resolution X-ray diffraction measurements, we observed a robust splitting of the lattice reflection into two coexisting peaks with distinct lattice constants at the CDW transition temperature TCDW, providing direct evidence for a first-order structural phase transition that is absent in samples with suppressed CDW order. Furthermore, the long-range CDW order was found to be only commensurate with lattice structures with the compressed out-of-plane lattice constant. The Landau free energy analysis shows that strong lattice-charge coupling is a key factor in stabilizing long-range CDW order. Our work clarifies the critical role of structural transformation in the CDW formation and opens opportunities for strain control of electronic phases in FeGe.

</details>


### [136] [Composition dependence of the critical Rayleigh number curve for macrosegregation in multicomponent metal alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20569)
*Owain S. Houghton,Adrian S. Sabau,Gregory B. Olson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 扩展Flemings模型，推导局部重熔引发通道形成的瑞利数判据，分析临界瑞利数与成分的关系


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究半固态糊状区对流不稳定性引发的通道形成机制，这类通道会导致斑点、通道偏析和A型偏析等缺陷，需要建立预测通道形成的判据

Method: 扩展Flemings模型，推导局部重熔引发通道形成的瑞利数Ra及其临界值Ra_crit的表达式，使用CALPHAD热物理数据评估这些表达式，通过SX-1镍基高温合金和Pb-Sn合金的基准实验和数值研究进行验证，并测试该判据与先前经验判据的相关性

Result: 发现Ra_crit随局部平均固相分数和多个热物理性质变化，由于这些性质在相对窄的成分范围内变化显著，表明Ra_crit是强烈依赖于成分的参数

Conclusion: 基于瑞利数的判据能够有效预测通道形成，临界瑞利数具有强烈的成分依赖性，这为预测铸造缺陷提供了理论依据

Abstract: Convective instabilities in the semi-solid mushy zone can trigger channel formation that leads to defects known as freckles, channel segregates and A-type segregates. In the present work, Flemings' model is extended to determine conditions for the onset of local remelting in an initially stagnant mushy zone. Expressions for the Rayleigh number $Ra$ and its critical value $Ra_{\rm crit}$, above which channels may form due to local remelting, are derived. Using thermophysical data from CALPHAD, these expressions are evaluated using results from benchmark experimental and numerical studies for the nickel-based superalloy SX-1 and Pb-Sn alloys. The correlation of this $Ra$-based criterion with previously reported empirical criteria is also tested for various steel compositions. It is found that $Ra_{\rm crit}$ varies with the local average solid fraction and several thermophysical properties. Since these properties can vary substantially within a relatively narrow composition range, it is suggested that $Ra_{\rm crit}$ is a strongly composition dependent parameter.

</details>


### [137] [Substrate-Mediated Evaporation and Stochastic Evolution of Supported Au Nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20635)
*Dmitri N. Zakharov,Xiaohui Qu,Hong Wang,Yuewei Lin,Aaron Stein,James P. Horwath,Shinjae Yoo,Eric A. Stach,Alexei V. Tkachenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过原位透射电镜和自动追踪技术，揭示了高温真空退火过程中支撑金纳米颗粒的集体质量交换机制，发现平均质量损失遵循尺寸无关的基底介导蒸发模式，而单个颗粒则表现出随机游走般的体积波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究支撑纳米颗粒在高温处理下的演化机制，特别是理解基底介导蒸发与集体质量交换之间的耦合作用，以及纳米尺度固有的随机性对颗粒演化的影响。

Method: 采用原位透射电镜结合自动追踪技术，在高温真空退火条件下实时监测支撑金纳米颗粒的演化；开发自洽理论模型，将基底介导蒸发与通过共享吸附原子场的集体2D Ostwald型质量交换相耦合；使用重整化屏蔽长度和背景浓度描述该过程；采用最小Langevin描述量化随机体积轨迹。

Result: 发现平均质量损失遵循平坦、几乎尺寸无关的基底介导蒸发曲线；单个纳米颗粒表现出显著的体积涨落，呈现随机游走轨迹；理论预测在实验相关区域存在近似尺寸无关的平均收缩速率，并阐明净质量损失如何抑制经典粗化过程；量化了随机体积轨迹的涨落谱，与间歇性吸附原子附着-脱离事件一致；同时表征了导致颗粒聚结的横向扩散运动。

Conclusion: 随机性是纳米尺度的固有特性，预测支撑纳米颗粒在早期和中期演化需要统一框架，结合基底介导蒸发、集体质量交换和随机涨落三个关键机制。

Abstract: We use in situ transmission electron microscopy with automated tracking to study supported gold nanoparticles (NPs) during high-temperature vacuum annealing. \rev{The average mass loss per NP is governed by a flat, nearly size-independent substrate-mediated evaporation profile.} On top of \rev{this mean shrinkage}, individual NPs show significant fluctuations in apparent growth or shrinkage, and NP volume follows a \rev{random-walk-like trajectory. To rationalize both the ensemble-mean behavior and the particle-resolved variability, we develop a self-consistent theory that couples substrate-mediated evaporation to collective 2D Ostwald-type mass exchange through a shared adatom field, described in terms of a renormalized screening length and background concentration. In the experimentally relevant regime, the theory predicts an approximately size-independent mean shrinkage rate and clarifies how net mass loss suppresses classical coarsening.} \rev{Superimposed on this deterministic drift, we quantify stochastic volume trajectories and capture their fluctuation spectrum with a minimal Langevin description consistent with intermittent adatom attachment and detachment events.} In addition, we characterize the lateral diffusive motion of NPs, which is responsible for their coalescence. Altogether, our results highlight that stochasticity is intrinsic at the nanoscale \rev{and that predicting the evolution of supported NPs at early and intermediate times requires a unified framework combining substrate-mediated evaporation, collective mass exchange, and stochastic fluctuations.

</details>


### [138] [Emerging hierarchical dislocation structures: Insights from scanning electron microscopy-electron backscatter diffraction in situ tensile testing and multifractal analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20689)
*Mikhail Lebyodkin,Maxim Gussev,Jamieson Brechtl,Tatiana Lebedkina*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 应用原位SEM/EBSD拉伸测试结合多重分形分析研究304L不锈钢在辐照前后变形诱导位错结构演化，发现辐照虽产生位错通道但基本层次结构相似，多重分形分析可定量表征位错空间复杂性和自组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解塑性变形过程中位错结构的演化对于预测金属材料的力学性能至关重要，特别是辐照对材料微观结构的影响需要定量表征方法。

Method: 采用原位扫描电子显微镜/电子背散射衍射拉伸测试结合多重分形分析，研究固溶退火304L不锈钢在原始状态和5.4dpa中子辐照状态下的变形诱导位错结构演化。

Result: 核平均取向差模式分析显示层次位错排列具有明确的多重分形标度行为；尽管辐照后出现位错通道等视觉差异，但奇异性谱表明两种状态产生相似的底层层次结构；多重分形分析定量揭示了辐照多晶材料中位错结构的加速形成和演化及其空间范围限制。

Conclusion: 辐照不仅改变微观结构，还改变相关性驱动的位错组织；多重分形分析是探测介观尺度变形机制的有力工具，可定量表征位错空间复杂性和自组织行为。

Abstract: Understanding the evolution of dislocation structures during plastic deformation is critical for predicting the mechanical performance of metallic materials. In this work, we applied in situ scanning electron microscopy/electron backscatter diffraction tensile testing combined with multifractal (MF) analysis to assess deformation-induced dislocation structure evolution in solution-annealed 304L stainless steel, both in its as-received and neutron-irradiated states (5.4 displacements per atom). The analysis of kernel average misorientation patterns revealed the formation of hierarchical dislocation arrangements that exhibit clear MF scaling behavior. Despite pronounced visual differences between nonirradiated and irradiated specimens -- most notably, the appearance of dislocation channels after irradiation -- the singularity spectra suggest that both conditions give rise to similar underlying hierarchical structures. MF analysis provides a quantitative measure of the spatial complexity and self-organization of dislocation patterns, highlighting the accelerated emergence and evolution of the dislocation structures in irradiated polycrystalline materials, as well as the limitation of their spatial extent. The findings indicate that irradiation not only modifies microstructure but also alters correlation-driven dislocation organization. More generally, they demonstrate that MF analysis is a powerful tool for probing mesoscale deformation mechanisms.

</details>


### [139] [Crystal Growth and anisotropic magneto-transport properties of semimetallic LaNiSb3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20709)
*Haribrahma Singh,Aarti Gautam,Prabuddha Kant Mishra,Rie Y. Umetsu,Ashok Kumar Ganguli*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: LaNiSb₃单晶通过Sn助熔剂法生长，具有正交Pbcm空间群结构，表现出各向异性多带电子输运特性，是研究拓扑半金属结构-性质关联的有前景候选材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LaNiSb₃的晶体结构和电子输运特性，探索其在拓扑半金属领域的潜在应用价值，特别是各向异性多带输运行为与结构-性质关联。

Method: 采用Sn助熔剂法生长LaNiSb₃单晶；通过结构表征确定晶体结构；在3-300K温度范围内进行电输运测量，包括电阻率、磁阻、角分辨磁阻和霍尔效应测量；使用半经典双带模型分析多带输运特性。

Result: LaNiSb₃结晶为正交Pbcm空间群；电阻率显示金属行为；磁阻表现出正各向异性响应，最大约8%（H∥b）；角分辨磁阻测量显示ab和ac平面内明显的二重对称性；霍尔测量表明高温下主要为电子型传导，低温下空穴贡献增加；违反Kohler标度律证实多带特性。

Conclusion: LaNiSb₃表现出各向异性多带电子输运特性，是研究拓扑半金属中结构-性质关联的有前景候选材料，其输运行为可通过半经典双带模型良好描述。

Abstract: Single crystals of LaNiSb$_3$ were grown using the Sn flux method. Structural characterization confirms that LaNiSb$_3$ crystallizes in the orthorhombic $Pbcm$ space group with lattice parameters $a = 13.0970(2)\,\mathrmÅ$, $b = 6.1400(4)\,\mathrmÅ$, and $c = 12.1270(4)\,\mathrmÅ$. Electrical resistivity measurements demonstrate metallic behavior over the entire temperature range of 3--300~K. The magnetoresistance exhibits a positive anisotropic response, attaining a maximum of $\sim 8\%$ for $H \parallel b$, with a pronounced crossover from quadratic to nearly linear field dependence. Angular-dependent MR measurements reveal a pronounced twofold symmetry upon magnetic field rotation within both the $ab$ and $ac$ crystallographic planes up to 50~K, indicating anisotropic charge transport. Hall resistivity measurements show predominantly electron-type conduction at high temperatures, with an increasing hole contribution upon cooling. The multiband character is further corroborated by the violation of Kohler's scaling and is well described within a semiclassical two-band framework. Collectively, these results suggest that LaNiSb$_3$ exhibits anisotropic multiband electronic transport and is a compelling candidate for exploring structure--property correlations in topological semimetals.

</details>


### [140] [Electric-field-induced X-ray Nonreciprocal Dichroism in Hematite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20737)
*Takeshi Hayashida,Koei Matsumoto,Keito Arakawa,Yves Joly,Sergio Di Matteo,Kenji Tamasaku,Yoshikazu Tanaka,Tsuyoshi Kimura*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过电场调控的X射线吸收谱，首次在赤铁矿中观测到时间反演奇性磁序的高阶多极子（磁四极子和磁环八极子）的物理表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赤铁矿作为典型的室温反铁磁体，其时间反演奇性磁结构虽然可以用高阶多极子描述，但这些多极子在可观测物理现象中的表现一直难以捉摸。研究旨在探索这些隐藏对称性在实验中的直接证据。

Method: 在Fe K-edge附近测量赤铁矿的X射线吸收谱，施加外电场以明确破坏空间反演对称性，观测电场诱导的X射线非互易线性二向色性。结合基于第一性原理密度泛函理论的数值模拟，并进行对称性分辨的多极子分析。

Result: 实验观测到电场诱导的X射线非互易线性二向色性，反映了磁序的时间反演奇性。数值模拟成功再现了观测谱线及其对反铁磁畴和X射线偏振的依赖性。多极子分析表明该响应源于电场诱导的磁四极子和磁环八极子。

Conclusion: 电场调控的X射线吸收为直接探测时间反演奇性反铁磁体中高阶多极子的反铁序提供了一种通用方法，建立了揭示磁性材料隐藏对称性性质的理论框架。

Abstract: Hematite (alpha-Fe2O3) is a prototypical room temperature antiferromagnet whose time-reversal-odd magnetic structure has recently attracted renewed attention. While such magnetic symmetry can be characterized in terms of higher-order multipoles beyond the magnetic dipole, their manifestation in measurable physical phenomena has remained largely elusive. In this work, we investigate x-ray absorption near the Fe K-edge of hematite under an applied electric field, which explicitly breaks space-inversion symmetry. We observe an electric-field-induced x-ray nonreciprocal linear dichroism (E-induced XNLD) that reflects the time-reversal-odd nature of the magnetic order. Numerical simulations based on ab-initio density functional theory reproduce the observed spectra, including their dependence on the antiferromagnetic domain and x-ray polarization. Furthermore, a symmetry-resolved multipole analysis reveals that this response originates from the magnetic quadrupole and the magnetic toroidal octupole induced by the applied electric field. These results demonstrate that electric-field-modulated x-ray absorption provides direct access to the antiferroic order of higher-order multipoles in time-reversal-odd antiferromagnets, thereby establishing a general framework to uncover hidden symmetry properties in magnetic materials.

</details>


### [141] [Temperature-dependent vibrational EELS simulations with nuclear quantum effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20744)
*Zuxian He,Ján Rusz*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 将热浴环形聚合物分子动力学（TRPMD）整合到时间自相关辅助波（TACAW）方法中，用于模拟低温下考虑核量子效应的振动电子能量损失谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低温条件下，振动光谱受核量子效应（特别是零点运动）主导，而传统的经典分子动力学无法准确描述这些效应。需要开发一种能够捕捉低温下核量子效应的理论工具，以定量分析新兴的低温扫描透射电子显微镜实验。

Method: 将热浴环形聚合物分子动力学（TRPMD）整合到时间自相关辅助波（TACAW）框架中。TACAW方法通过推导束波函数的时间自相关来计算散射强度，TRPMD则用于模拟包含核量子效应的原子动力学，从而在低温振动电子能量损失谱中自然包含零点运动等量子效应。

Result: 核量子效应导致硅在低温下的振动光谱与经典分子动力学预测存在显著偏差。TRPMD-TACAW方法正确预测了硅中光学声子峰强度几乎与温度无关的特性，这与第一玻恩近似一致。

Conclusion: TRPMD-TACAW方法为探测振动电子能量损失谱的低温极限提供了稳健的理论工具，为定量分析新兴的低温扫描透射电子显微镜实验提供了必要的基准。

Abstract: The Time Autocorrelation of Auxiliary Wave (TACAW) method has established a framework for modeling angle-resolved electron energy loss spectroscopy (EELS) of phonons and magnons by deriving scattering intensities from the time autocorrelation of the beam wavefunction. This approach enables efficient computation of scattering intensities while naturally accounting for dynamical diffraction and multiple-scattering effects. In the cryogenic regime, vibrational spectra are dominated by nuclear quantum effects, notably zero-point motion. To capture these effects in low-temperature vibrational EELS, we incorporate thermostatted ring polymer molecular dynamics (TRPMD) into the TACAW formalism. Our results demonstrate that nuclear quantum effects lead to significant deviations from classical molecular dynamics predictions in the vibrational spectra of silicon at low temperatures and correctly predict the nearly temperature-independent optical phonon peak intensities in silicon, consistent with the first Born approximation. The TRPMD-TACAW method provides a robust theoretical tool for probing the low-temperature limit of vibrational EELS, offering a necessary benchmark for the quantitative analysis of emerging cryogenic scanning transmission electron microscopy experiments.

</details>


### [142] [From Photons to Electrons: Accelerated Materials Discovery via Random Libraries and Automated Scanning Transmission Electron Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20858)
*Boris Slautin,Kamyar Barakati,Utkarsh Pratiush,Christopher D. Lowe,Catherine C. Bodinger,Brandi M. Cossairt,Mahshid Ahmadi,Austin Houston,Timur Bazhirov,Kamal Choudhary,Gerd Duscher,Sergei Kalinin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出从光子基表征转向电子基表征的范式转变，利用STEM电子显微镜和机器学习实现高通量材料发现，相比传统XRD方法能实现数量级更高的组合空间覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料预测算法在实际应用中受到表征瓶颈的限制，传统光子基探测技术（如XRD或拉曼）采集时间长、访问延迟高，限制了材料发现只能探索准三元组成空间。

Method: 提出从光子基到电子基表征的范式转变，制定STEM化学和结构表征的成本函数和探索策略，使用蒙特卡洛模拟验证随机化学库方法，并在实验室STEM平台上实现基于机器学习的自主发现系统。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟显示，随机化学库方法相比传统扩散库/X射线方法，能在高维组成和相空间中实现数量级更高的有效覆盖。在实验室STEM平台上成功实现了无需人工干预的自主发现。

Conclusion: 电子基、机器学习赋能的STEM为组合丰富的材料发现提供了可扩展的途径，通过保留组成和处理元数据的标记或位置编码库，能够实现组成和工艺空间的联合探索。

Abstract: The real-world implementation of materials prediction algorithms remains limited by persistent characterization bottlenecks in materials discovery, where photon-based probe techniques (e.g., XRD or Raman) impose long acquisition times and access latencies, restricting exploration to quasi-ternary composition spaces typically realized as compositional libraries. Here, we argue that a paradigm shift from photon- to electron-based characterization can realign materials characterization with modern high-throughput synthesis. We formulate cost functions and exploration strategies for STEM-based chemical and structural characterization and use Monte Carlo simulations to show that random chemical libraries, where compositionally distinct regions are co-located within a single specimen and interrogated in situ by electron spectroscopies, can sample high-dimensional composition and phase spaces with orders-of-magnitude greater effective coverage than conventional spread-library/X-ray approaches. We further demonstrate autonomous discovery on a laboratory STEM platform, where ML-based autotuning and scripted control enable iterative region selection and characterization without human intervention. Finally, we outline extensions to labeled or position-encoded libraries that preserve compositional and processing metadata, enabling joint exploration of composition and process spaces. Together, these results establish electron-based, ML-enabled STEM as a scalable pathway toward combinatorially rich materials discovery.

</details>


### [143] [High Entropy Alloy under Shock Compression: Optical-Pump X-Ray-Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21076)
*Hsin Hui Huang,Meguya Ryu,Shuji Kamegaki,Dominyka Stonyte,Tadas Malinauskas,Yoshiaki Nishijima,Rosalie Hocking,Nguyen Hoai An Le,Tomas Katkus,Haoran Mu,Soon Hock Ng,Samuel Pinches,Andrew S. M. Ang,Vygantas Mizeikis,Nadia Zatsepin,Kohei Miyanishi,Toshinori Yabuuchi,Hirotaka Nakamura,Alexis Amouretti,Norimasa Ozaki,Tommaso Vinci,Arturas Vailionis,Eugene G. Gamaly,Damien G. Hicks,Junko Morikawa,Saulius Juodkazis*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过激光冲击实验结合XFEL时间分辨X射线衍射，首次探索了两种高熵合金（CuPdAgPtAu和CrFeCoNiCuMo）在极端动态条件下的瞬态相变行为，观测到高达5.1%的晶格压缩和0.3 ns的瞬态相存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高熵合金因其可定制的机械性能而备受工程关注，但其在极端动态条件（如冲击加载）下的基本行为尚未被探索。本研究旨在填补这一空白，通过先进的实验技术研究高熵合金在冲击加载下的瞬态相变和动态响应。

Method: 采用激光冲击实验技术，将532 nm、5 ns、16 J的高强度激光脉冲聚焦于覆盖在25 μm厚黑色Kapton烧蚀层上的1 μm厚高熵合金微薄膜。使用12 keV、7 fs的X射线自由电子激光脉冲进行时间分辨X射线衍射探测，同时采用速度干涉仪系统进行原位速度监测。

Result: 在CuPdAgPtAu高熵合金中观测到沿(111)晶面高达5.1%的晶格压缩的瞬态相，该相存在时间为0.3 ns。阻抗匹配Hugoniot分析估计冲击压力为55±6 GPa，而基于Au和Fe的状态方程模型预测自由表面压力为80 GPa。自由表面最大速度达到约5 km/s。

Conclusion: 该研究成功展示了高熵合金样品制备和基于XFEL的X射线衍射表征在极端冲击加载条件下的适用性，为这类新兴材料的状态方程实验测定提供了有前景的技术途径，为进一步系统研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: High entropy alloys (HEAs) are multi-principal-element alloys designed for tailorable mechanical performance and have been attracting significant engineering interest, yet their fundamental behaviour under extreme dynamic conditions, such as shock loading, remains unexplored. Here, we report laser-shock experiments on two different types of 1-micrometers-thick HEA microfilms, CuPdAgPtAu and CrFeCoNiCuMo, on 25-micrometers-thick black-Kapton ablator driven by a high intensity laser pulse (532 nm, 5 ns, 16 J, 0.5-mm diameter focal spot) and probed by an X-ray free electron laser (XFEL) pulse (12 keV, 7 fs). Time-resolved X-ray diffraction (XRD) shows the formation of a transient phase with a lattice compression up to 5.1% of the CuPdAgPtAu HEA along the (111) plane; this transient compressed phase existed for 0.3 ns. The impedance matching Hugoniot analysis estimated a shock pressure of 55 +/- 6 GPa in the HEA film, while Au- and Fe-based equations of state (EoS) modelling predict 80 GPa (0.8 MBar) at the free HEA surface. The free HEA surface reached maximum velocities of ~ 5 km/s as recorded from in situ monitoring with the velocity interferometry system for any reflector (VISAR) imaging. These initial HEA results show the suitability of HEA sample preparation and XFEL-based XRD characterisation under extreme shock loading, and are promising for experimental determination of the EoS of this emerging class of materials (beamtime proposal No.: 2024A8503 for a 6-hour preliminary experiment).

</details>


### [144] [Unlocking Static Polarization and Strain Density Waves in Perovskites by Softening a Hidden Antiferrodistortive Tilt Gradient Mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21120)
*Yajun Zhang,Devesh R. Kripalani,Xu He,Konstantin Shapovalov,Jiyuan Yang,Hongjian Zhao,Shi Liu,Huadong Yong,Xingyi Zhang,Jie Wang,Kun Zhou,Philippe Ghosez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文发现了一种在钙钛矿材料中实现静态极化密度波和应变密度波的新策略，通过对称性分析和第一性原理计算，揭示了SrTiO3和SrMnO3中新型Pmn21相的存在，并展示了应变密度波如何通过挠磁效应激活可电调谐的自旋密度波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极化密度波和应变密度波作为空间调制序的基本范式，在平衡态中一直难以实现。研究旨在开发一种通用的对称性驱动策略，在钙钛矿材料中解锁这些静态密度波，以探索新的电机械和磁电功能。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算结合群论分析，研究了SrTiO3和SrMnO3在适度拉伸应变下的声子色散。发现了Ima2基态中先前被忽视的小q波矢软反铁畸变倾斜梯度模式，并分析了其与硬极性声学声子的三线性耦合机制。

Result: 发现了从Ima2相到新型低能Pmn21相的结构转变，该相具有长程有序的极化密度波和应变密度波。特别地，在SrMnO3中工程化的应变密度波通过挠磁效应激活了可电调谐的自旋密度波。

Conclusion: 该研究从根本上修正了典型钙钛矿的应变-相图，建立了一个统一的声子工程框架，将调制声子不稳定性与目标密度波序联系起来，为设计先进的电机械和磁电功能提供了新途径。

Abstract: Spin density waves (SDWs) represent a fundamental paradigm of spatially modulated order in condensed matter systems, yet their electrical and mechanical analogues polarization and strain density waves (PDWs and StDWs) have remained elusive as equilibrium phases. Here, we introduce a general, symmetry-driven strategy to unlock static PDWs and StDWs in perovskites SrTiO3 and SrMnO3. Using first-principles calculations, we uncover a previously overlooked soft antiferrodistortive tilt gradient mode at small-q wavevector in the phonon dispersion of their presumed Ima2 ground state under moderate tensile strain. Group-theory analysis reveals that a hard polaracoustic phonon, which intrinsically carries PDWs and StDWs, is improperly destabilized by a trilinear coupling with this modulated tilt mode and an inherently uniform tilt mode. This interaction drives a structural transition from the Ima2 phase to a novel lower-energy Pmn21 phase that hosts long-range-ordered PDWs and StDWs. Strikingly, the engineered StDWs in SrMnO3 activate an electrically tunable SDW via the flexomagnetic effect. These discoveries fundamentally revise the strain-phase diagrams of prototypical perovskites and establish a unified phonon-engineering framework that links modulated phonon instabilities to targeted density-wave order, offering new pathways for designing advanced electromechanical and magnetoelectric functionalities.

</details>


### [145] [Framework for Quasiperiodic Interfaces: Proximal Coincidence Point Set and Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21158)
*Suining Xiong,Wenwen Zou,Pingwen Zhang,Kai Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出统一理论框架连接数学准周期性与经典晶体学模型，通过投影方法解析界面结构中的准周期特征


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立数学准周期性与经典晶体学模型之间的桥梁，为界面结构提供严格的理论解释，并扩展对非公度结构的建模能力

Method: 基于严格的截断投影构造，提出近邻重合点集理论，结合Landau-Brazovskii模型和投影方法，分析界面准周期有序性

Result: 揭示了代表性准周期特征：BCC[110]倾斜晶界中的广义Fibonacci序列，低角度BCC[100]扭转晶界中的重复图案，以及高角度BCC[100]扭转晶界中出现的12重和8重准晶体

Conclusion: 该框架不仅为界面结构提供了严格的理论解释，还为建模其他类型的非公度结构提供了路径，成功连接了数学准周期性与经典晶体学模型

Abstract: We present a unified theoretical and computational framework that bridges mathematical quasiperiodicity with classical crystallographic models. Based on a rigorous cut-and-projection construction, the proposed proximal coincidence point set (PCPS) theory extends the classical coincidence site lattice model and further incorporates physically motivated perturbations encoding interfacial atomic mobility as well as visual indistinguishability. Spectral characteristics of PCPS naturally motivate a conserved Landau-Brazovskii model combined with projection method, yielding unified high accuracy in resolving quasiperiodic order across the entire interfacial plane. Representative quasiperiodic features are revealed in our numerical results, including generalized Fibonacci sequences in BCC [110] tilt GBs, as well as repetitive patterns within the interstices of dislocation networks in low-angle BCC [100] twist GBs and phase boundaries between BCC and face-centered cubic crystals. In high-angle BCC [100] twist GBs, 12- and 8-fold quasicrystals emerge, while the PCPS theory combined with cyclotomic field projections further explains their restrictions of non-crystallographic symmetries. This framework not only provides a rigorous theoretical explanation for interface structures but also offers a path toward modeling other types of incommensurate structures.

</details>


### [146] [First Plasma Atomic Layer Etching of Diamond via O$_2$/Kr Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21159)
*Duc Duy Tran,Cedric Mannequin,Aboulaye Traore,Masahiro Sasaki,Etienne Gheeraert*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 首次报道了金刚石的等离子体原子层刻蚀（ALE）工艺，采用氧表面改性和氪离子去除的循环等离子体序列，实现了原子级精度的可控材料去除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种能够实现原子级精度、低损伤的金刚石刻蚀工艺，以满足功率电子、光子学、量子传感和量子计算等先进器件制造的需求。

Method: 在电感耦合等离子体反应器中实施循环等离子体序列：交替进行O₂等离子体暴露和低能Kr离子轰击两个分离步骤。

Result: 实现了每循环6.85Å的刻蚀深度，刻蚀后的金刚石表面粗糙度低于原始材料，XPS分析证实金刚石键合结构得以保留，表明基本无损伤刻蚀。

Conclusion: 基于O₂/Kr化学的等离子体ALE为金刚石提供了可行的损伤可控纳米级加工途径，为先进器件制造开辟了新机遇。

Abstract: We report the first plasma atomic layer etching (ALE) process for diamond using a cyclic plasma sequence composed of two separated steps: oxygen surface modification and krypton ion removal. The process is implemented in an inductively coupled plasma reactor using alternating O$_2$ plasma exposure and low-energy Kr ion bombardment.
  This cyclic process exhibits the characteristic self-limiting behavior of ALE and enables controlled material removal with atomic-scale precision. An etch depth per cycle of \SI{6.85}{\angstrom} was achieved. Surface analysis reveals that the etched diamond surfaces exhibit lower roughness than the pristine material, while XPS confirms the preservation of the diamond bonding structure and indicates essentially damage-free etching.
  These results demonstrate that plasma ALE based on O$_2$/Kr chemistry provides a viable route toward damage-controlled nanoscale processing of diamond, opening new opportunities for advanced device fabrication in power electronics, photonics, quantum sensing and quantum computing technologies.

</details>


### [147] [Disentangling Anomalous Hall Effect Mechanisms and Extra Symmetry Protection in Altermagnetic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21246)
*Yuansheng Bu,Ziyin Song,Zhong Fang,Quansheng Wu,Hongming Weng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过构建紧束缚模型和对称性分析，揭示了反铁磁系统中反常霍尔电导随自旋倾斜角演化的两种机制：传统反常霍尔效应和晶体霍尔效应，并发现了一个被静态磁群分析忽略的隐藏C110旋转对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究反常霍尔电导在共面共线反铁磁系统中随自旋倾斜角变化的演化规律，区分传统反常霍尔效应和晶体霍尔效应两种不同起源的横向输运机制。

Method: 构建基于体心四方晶格中三个t2g轨道的紧束缚模型，包含第三近邻跳跃项以准确描述交变磁性材料的能带分裂特征。采用基于自旋空间群的对称性分析，将自旋轨道耦合作为微扰处理，理论区分并数值验证两种横向输运机制。

Result: 发现两种机制驱动的电导分量遵循不同的三角函数依赖关系。识别出一个在静态磁群分析中被忽略的隐藏C110旋转对称性，该对称性连接不同倾斜角的磁构型，并严格保护共线系统中正交电导分量的等价性。

Conclusion: 通过自旋取向矢量展开反常霍尔电导，揭示了隐藏对称性在连接不同磁构型中的桥梁作用，为理解反铁磁系统中反常霍尔效应的起源和对称性保护机制提供了新见解。

Abstract: We investigate the evolution of Anomalous Hall Conductivity (AHC) in a coplanar and collinear antiferromagnetic system with varying spin canting angles. A tight-binding model based on three t2g-orbitals in a body-centered tetragonal lattice is constructed, where the inclusion of third-nearest neighbor hopping is demonstrated to be essential for capturing the characteristic energy band splitting of altermagnetic materials. By employing a symmetry analysis based on spin space groups and treating spin-orbit coupling (SOC) as a perturbation, we theoretically distinguish and numerically verify two origins of the transverse transport: the conventional anomalous Hall effect (AHE) induced by net magnetization and the Crystal Hall Effect (CHE) arising from specific crystal symmetries. Our results show that the conductivity components driven by these two mechanisms follow distinct trigonometric dependencies on the canting angle. Crucially, we identify a hidden C110 rotational symmetry that has been previously overlooked in static magnetic group analyses. By expanding the AHC in terms of spin orientation vectors, we demonstrate that this symmetry acts as a bridge connecting distinct magnetic configurations with different canting angles, thereby strictly protecting the equivalence of orthogonal conductivity components in the collinear system.

</details>


### [148] [Engineering magnetism in hybrid organic-inorganic metal halide perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21318)
*Yaiza Asensio,Lucía Olano-Vegas,Samuele Mattioni,Marco Gobbi,Fèlix Casanova,Luis E. Hueso,Beatriz Martín-García*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 综述文章系统总结了含过渡金属的有机-无机杂化金属卤化物钙钛矿的磁性研究进展，包括合成方法、磁性行为、调控策略及其在磁光电子学和自旋电子学中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有机-无机杂化金属卤化物钙钛矿（HOIPs）具有化学和结构灵活性，为调控其光学和磁性性质提供了理想平台。本文旨在从磁性角度重新审视HOIPs，探索其作为按需设计的低维磁性材料的潜力，特别是含过渡金属的HOIPs。

Method: 采用综述研究方法：1) 介绍HOIPs的组成和晶体结构；2) 总结磁性HOIPs的合成方法；3) 系统分析其丰富的磁性行为和现象学；4) 探讨磁性起源及通过改变钙钛矿相、化学组成和维度进行调控的指导原则；5) 展示在磁光电子学和自旋电子学中的应用潜力。

Result: 全面呈现了含过渡金属HOIPs磁性研究的进展：总结了合成方法、磁性行为特征（如铁磁性、反铁磁性、自旋玻璃态等）、磁性起源机制（如超交换、双交换、Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用等），以及通过结构调控实现磁性性质定制的方法。

Conclusion: 含过渡金属的HOIPs展现出作为新型低维磁性材料的巨大潜力，但在器件集成等方面仍面临挑战。未来研究方向包括从磁性掺杂转向纯过渡金属基HOIPs，这将推动该领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: The chemical and structural flexibility of hybrid organic-inorganic metal halide perovskites (HOIPs) provides an ideal platform for engineering not only their well-studied optical properties, but also their magnetic ones. In this review we present HOIPs from a new perspective, turning the attention to their magnetic properties and their potential as new class of on-demand low-dimensional magnetic materials. Focusing on HOIPs containing transition metals, we comprehensively present the progress that has been made in preparing, understanding and exploring magnetic HOIPs. First, we briefly introduce HOIPs in terms of composition and crystal structure and examine the synthesis protocols commonly used to prepare those showing magnetic properties. Then, we present their rich magnetic behavior and phenomenology; discuss their origin and guidelines for tuning them by changing the perovskite phase, chemical composition and dimensionality; and showcase their potential application in magneto-optoelectronics and spintronics. Finally, we describe the current challenges in the field, such as their integration into devices, as well as the emerging possibilities of moving from magnetic doping to pure transition metal-based HOIPs, which will motivate further studies in the future.

</details>


### [149] [Improved cycling stability and lithium utilization in trilayer Al-LLZO revealed by Electrochemical cycling performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21578)
*Naisargi Kanabar,Seiichiro Higashiya,Haralabos Efstathiadis*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过制备致密-梯度三层Al-LLZO固体电解质，显著提升了全固态电池性能，放电容量比纯致密结构提高近一倍，界面电阻降低且稳定性改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石榴石型Al-LLZO固体电解质虽有潜力，但界面电阻限制了其在全固态电池中的应用，需要改善界面性能以提升电池性能。

Method: 制备致密-梯度三层Al-LLZO电解质（多孔-致密-多孔结构），组装Li/Al-LLZO/NMC(111)全电池，通过电化学测试、EIS、SEM和NRA进行表征分析。

Result: 三层结构电池25次循环后放电容量约55 mAhg⁻¹，是纯致密结构（约27 mAhg⁻¹）的近两倍；初始界面电阻约373 Ω，稳定性改善；NRA显示近表面锂含量提升至约75%（致密结构约48%）。

Conclusion: 微观结构梯度化能有效改善锂分布，降低界面电阻，提升全固态电池的电化学性能，为高性能固体电解质设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Garnet-type Li$_{6.25}$Al$_{0.25}$La$_3$Zr$_2$O$_{12}$ (Al-LLZO) solid electrolytes are promising for all-solid-state batteries but are limited by interfacial resistance. In this work, dense and graded tri-layer Al-LLZO electrolytes were fabricated and tested in Li/Al-LLZO/NMC(111) full cells. After 25 cycles, the tri-layer cell delivered discharge capacity of $\sim$55 mAhg$^{-1}$, nearly twice that of the dense Al-LLZO ($\sim$27 mAhg$^{-1}$). EIS showed lower initial interfacial resistance ($\sim$373 $Ω$) and improved stability. SEM confirmed a porous-dense-porous structure, while NRA revealed enhanced near-surface lithium ($\sim$75%) compared to dense Al-LLZO ($\sim$48%). These results highlight the role of microstructural grading in improving lithium distribution and cell performance.

</details>


### [150] [Ideal band structures for high-performance thermoelectric materials with band convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21649)
*Yuya Hattori,Hidetomo Usui,Yoshikazu Mizuguchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过虚拟光谱电导模型分析带收敛系统中的最优能带结构，提出了实现高热电优值zT的定量设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究热电材料中能带结构工程的设计原则，为开发高性能热电材料提供理论指导和定量设计准则。

Method: 采用基于虚拟光谱电导模型的数值计算，研究双抛物线能带系统，其中多个能带参数可独立控制。

Result: 提出了四条关键设计原则：1)远离化学势的能带贡献可忽略；2)抑制双极效应需要足够大的带隙；3)带收敛系统中能带边缘能量分离应接近零；4)高光谱电导率对高zT至关重要。

Conclusion: 该研究为热电材料的能带结构工程提供了物理透明且定量的设计指导，有助于开发多种高性能热电材料。

Abstract: We investigate optimal band structures in band-converged systems to achieve high zT using numerical calculations based on a virtual spectral conductivity model. We consider a two parabolic band system, in which multiple band parameters can be independently controlled. Despite its simplicity, this model provides theoretical validation of empirical trends observed in thermoelectric materials. Our results provide a physically transparent set of design principles for band-structure engineering, offering quantitative design guidelines for the development of a wide range of thermoelectric materials. The main conclusions are as follows: (i) When a band does not cross the chemical potential and |μ-E_edge |>5k_B T, the contribution of the band to zT is negligibly small; (ii) To suppress the bipolar effect, a band gap E_g satisfying E_g>5k_B T_op, where T_op is the operating temperature, is required; (iii) In band-converged systems, the energy separation between the band edge ΔE should satisfy ΔE~0 to maximize zT when interband scattering is insignificant; (iv) Achieving high spectral conductivity Σ (high band degeneracy N, density of states effective mass m_DOS^*, and relaxation time τ) near the band edge is essential for achieving high zT.

</details>


### [151] [SAM Molecular Stacking with Heterogeneous Orientationfor High-Performance Perovskite Photovoltaics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21657)
*Lei Huang,Kai-Li Wang,Zhang Chen,Zhen-Huang,Saidjafar Murodzoda,Xin Chen,Jing Chen,Chun-Hao Chen,Yu Xia,Yu-Tong Yang,Jia-Cheng Li,Dilshod Nematov,Ilhan Yavuz,Zhao-Kui Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 热蒸发自组装单层膜（eSAM）通过厚度控制形成分子取向梯度，解决了溶液法SAM的均匀性问题，显著提升钙钛矿光伏器件的效率和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 溶液法制备的自组装单层膜（SAM）存在均匀性差的问题，这成为钙钛矿光伏器件规模化生产的主要瓶颈。需要开发一种能够实现均匀空穴传输且适合工业化生产的SAM制备方法。

Method: 采用热蒸发技术制备自组装单层膜（eSAM），通过精确控制厚度实现分子取向的调控。研究发现，较厚的eSAM会自发形成从垂直取向到水平取向的梯度结构，这种梯度分子堆积形成了递减的能量势垒。

Result: eSAM形成的梯度分子取向结构显著改善了空穴传输效率，同时确保了优异的表面覆盖度并引导高质量钙钛矿薄膜的生长。器件性能方面：全真空蒸发器件的小面积（0.108 cm²）效率达到21.46%，大面积模块（15.52 cm²）效率为19.38%；基于eSAM但采用溶液法钙钛矿的器件效率达到23.67%。

Conclusion: 热蒸发SAM技术不仅解决了溶液法SAM的均匀性问题，还通过梯度分子取向结构实现了更高效的空穴传输。这种技术为钙钛矿光伏器件的工业化生产提供了可行路径，具有重要的商业化前景。

Abstract: This study demonstrates that thermal-evaporated SAM (eSAM) films, particularly in a thick configuration, spontaneously adopt a heterogeneous molecular orientation, forming a vertical-to-horizontal gradient in molecular packing. This unique architecture establishes a graded energy barrier, which is shown to facilitate more efficient hole transport compared with the single energy barrier presented by conventional thin SAMs. In conclusion, while solution-processed SAMs present formidable scalability challenges, the thermal evaporation of SAMs offers a viable pathway toward industrial-scale fabrication. The strategy of employing thick eSAM films with gradient molecular packing not only circumvents the uniformity issues of solution methods but also introduces a superior structure for charge transport, positioning it as a promising enabler for the commercialization of high-efficiency perovskite photovoltaics. The inability to achieve uniform hole transport with solution-processed self-assembled monolayers (SAMs) constitutes a fundamental bottleneck for scaling perovskite photovoltaics. Herein, we demonstrate that thermal-evaporated SAMs (eSAMs) overcome this limitation by enabling precise thickness control. Crucially, a thickened eSAM spontaneously forms a vertical-to-horizontal gradient in molecular orientation, which creates a descending energy barrier that directionally facilitates hole transport. This tailored interface also ensures excellent surface coverage and directs the growth of high-quality perovskite films. Consequently, the resultant photovoltaic devices set new benchmarks, delivering impressive power conversion efficiencies (PCEs) of 21.46% (small-area, 0.108 cm2) and 19.38% (large-area module, 15.52 cm2) for fully vacuum-evaporated devices, while also setting an impressive PCE of 23.67% for eSAM-based devices with solution-processed perovskites.

</details>


### [152] [Exploring self-driving labs for optoelectronic materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21704)
*Jonathan Staaf Scragg*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 论文提出探索驱动型自主实验室新范式，专注于生成用于数据驱动科学的材料缺陷物理数据集，而非传统优化驱动型自主实验室的性能优化目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数自主实验室采用优化驱动范式，专注于快速收敛于性能指标，但缺乏对合成-性能关系背后物理机制的深入理解。需要一种新的科学自主实验室范式，专注于生成能够揭示材料缺陷物理的数据集。

Method: 提出探索驱动型自主实验室设计原则：系统扰动热力学和动力学合成变量，并行测量缺陷敏感观测值，生成结构化数据集。以Cu2ZnSn(S,Se)4无机光电材料为案例研究，展示缺陷感知材料探索的规模。

Result: 论文阐述了科学自主实验室的设计框架，能够生成可转移、可重用的数据集，提供对光电材料缺陷物理的深入洞察。通过案例研究揭示了当前范式的不足和缺陷感知材料探索的复杂性。

Conclusion: 适当设计的自主实验室能够生成必要的结构化数据集，实现机制推断并推进合成感知的材料设计。探索驱动型自主实验室为数据驱动材料科学提供了新范式，专注于生成科学洞察而非单纯性能优化。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories (SDLs), by combining automation with machine learning-guided experiment selection, have the potential to transform experimental materials science. To date, most SDLs have been optimisation-driven, designed to rapidly converge on performance metrics, by embedding multiple mechanistic layers within platform-specific surrogate models. Such approaches excel at process tuning yet offer limited insight into the underlying physics governing synthesis-property relationships. Here we articulate a complementary paradigm: the exploration-driven, or scientific, SDL, whose primary purpose is the generation of data for data-driven science. We exemplify this concept for the case of inorganic optoelectronic materials, arguing that defect physics, which forms the central mechanistic link between synthesis conditions and functional properties, provides the foundation for designing a suitable SDL. Because defect populations and their spatial organisation cannot generally be resolved directly - nor fully predicted from first principles - the task of the SDL is to generate datasets in which thermodynamic and kinetic synthesis variables are systematically perturbed and defect-sensitive observables measured in parallel. From this basis, we propose a set of design principles for scientific SDLs that will enable them to operate close to the physics of optoelectronic materials, thereby generating transferrable and reusable datasets offering radical insight. We use Cu2ZnSn(S,Se)4 as a case study, both to show the scale of the task of defect-aware materials exploration as well to highlight as the deficiencies in the current paradigm. We propose that properly designed SDLs can generate the structured datasets necessary to enable mechanistic inference and advance synthesis-aware materials design.

</details>


### [153] [Electric toroidal octupolar symmetry in pyrite FeS$_2$ probed by Raman optical activity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21756)
*Yuki Suganuma,Gakuto Kusuno,Hikaru Watanabe,Rikuto Oiwa,Hitoshi Mori,Ryotaro Arita,Takuya Satoh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在黄铁矿FeS₂中首次观测到拉曼光学活性，该材料具有电环形八极对称性。在交叉圆偏振下，相邻{111}晶面之间观察到明显的圆偏振强度差符号反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索具有高阶轴向多极对称性材料的拉曼光学活性，建立拉曼光学活性作为探测高阶轴向多极对称性的新探针。

Method: 通过实验观测黄铁矿FeS₂的拉曼光学活性，特别关注交叉圆偏振条件下相邻{111}晶面的圆偏振强度差符号反转。结合对称性分析和第一性原理计算验证实验结果。

Result: 在黄铁矿FeS₂中观察到清晰的拉曼光学活性信号，仅在双重简并的Eg声子模式中出现，其他模式中不存在。相邻{111}晶面之间观察到可重复的圆偏振强度差符号反转。第一性原理计算成功复现了这些特征。

Conclusion: 拉曼光学活性可作为探测高阶轴向多极对称性的有效探针，黄铁矿FeS₂中观察到的现象为研究具有复杂对称性的材料提供了新方法。

Abstract: We report Raman optical activity in pyrite FeS$_2$, which hosts an electric toroidal octupolar symmetry. A clear and reproducible sign reversal of the circular intensity difference is observed between neighboring $\{111\}$ faces under cross-circular polarization. The signal appears only for the doubly degenerate $E_g$ phonon mode and is absent for other modes, consistent with symmetry analysis. First-principles calculations reproduce these features, establishing Raman optical activity as a probe of higher-rank axial multipolar symmetry.

</details>


### [154] [A Unified Heterogeneous Implementation of Numerical Atomic Orbitals-Based Real-Time TDDFT within the ABACUS Package](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21835)
*Taoni Bao,Yuanbo Li,Zichao Deng,Haotian Zhao,Denghui Lu,Yike Huang,Chao Lian,Lixin He,Mohan Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: ABACUS包中基于数值原子轨道的实时含时密度泛函理论统一异构计算框架，通过三层抽象设计加速波函数传播和哈密顿量构建，在单GPU上相比双路CPU节点获得显著加速，支持数十GPU的高效并行扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个高性能、可移植的平台，用于大规模第一性原理超快电子动力学模拟，解决基于数值原子轨道的RT-TDDFT计算中计算密集部分（实时波函数传播和实空间网格操作）的性能瓶颈问题。

Method: 提出三层协同设计的抽象层：统一数据容器、统一线性代数算子和统一网格积分接口。这些层共同加速NAO-based RT-TDDFT的两个最耗计算部分：显式实时波函数传播以及哈密顿量构建和外场下力评估等实空间网格操作。

Result: 通过计算从有限分子到周期性固体的光学性质验证方法，与标准基准测试结果高度一致。硅块体性能评估显示单GPU相比完全利用的双路CPU节点获得显著的挂钟时间加速。分布式多GPU强扩展测试证实了在数十个GPU上的高并行效率。

Conclusion: 这项工作建立了一个用于大规模第一性原理超快电子动力学模拟的高性能、可移植平台，为RT-TDDFT计算提供了有效的异构计算解决方案，支持从分子到固体系统的光学性质计算。

Abstract: We present a unified heterogeneous computing framework for real-time time-dependent density functional theory (RT-TDDFT) based on numerical atomic orbitals (NAOs), implemented in the ABACUS package. We introduce three co-designed abstraction layers, including unified data containers, unified linear algebra operators, and unified grid integration interfaces. These layers collectively accelerate the two most demanding parts of NAO-based RT-TDDFT: explicit real-time wavefunction propagation and real-space grid operations such as Hamiltonian construction and force evaluation under external fields. We validate the method by computing optical properties for systems ranging from finite molecules to periodic solids, showing excellent agreement with standard benchmarks. Performance evaluations on bulk silicon demonstrate that a single GPU can achieve substantial wall-clock speedup over a fully utilized dual-socket CPU node. Furthermore, distributed multi-GPU strong-scaling tests confirm high parallel efficiency over tens of GPUs. This work establishes a high-performance, portable platform for large-scale first-principles simulations of ultrafast electron dynamics.

</details>


### [155] [Magnetocaloric Effect of Pure and Diluted Quantum Magnet Yb$_3$Ga$_5$O$_{12}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21922)
*E. Riordan,E. Lhotel,N. -R. Camara,C. Marin,M. E. Zhitomirsky*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 量子偶极磁体Yb3Ga5O12及其非磁性Y取代样品(Yb1-xYx)3Ga5O12的磁热效应研究，发现适度稀释(x=0.2)样品在低场下磁热效应增强，为低温磁制冷应用提供新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子偶极磁体Yb3Ga5O12及其非磁性取代样品的磁热效应，探索通过化学取代改善材料热导率而不损失制冷能力的可能性，为低温磁制冷应用开发新材料。

Method: 对纯单晶(x=0)和粉末样品(x=0.2, 0.4)在70 mK-300 K温度范围和最高8 T磁场下进行磁化测量，从磁化数据推导磁熵变ΔS_m，并通过居里-外斯定律拟合分析磁相关性。

Result: x=0.2样品的体积熵变与纯化合物相当，在低场下甚至增强；x=0.4样品磁热效应减弱，符合传统稀释模型。稀释样品均显示正居里温度，表明铁磁相关性持续存在。

Conclusion: 适度稀释的YbGG基材料在保持磁热效应的同时，可通过化学取代改善热导率，为解决低温磁制冷中的热导率挑战提供了有前景的途径，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: The magnetocaloric effect in the quantum dipolar magnet Yb$_3$Ga$_5$O$_{12}$ is studied both for pure material and with non-magnetic substitution: (Yb$_{1-x}$Y$_x$)$_3$Ga$_5$O$_{12}$. Magnetization measurements have been performed on a single crystal, $x=0$, and on powder samples with $x = 0.2$ and 0.4 in the temperature range between 70 mK to 300 K and in magnetic fields up to 8 T. The magnetic entropy change $ΔS_m$, a key figure of merit for adiabatic demagnetization refrigeration, has been derived from the magnetization data. The $x=0.2$ sample exhibits the volumetric entropy variation comparable to, and at low fields even enhanced relative to, the pure compound. In contrast, the 40\%\ diluted sample shows a reduced effect, consistent with the conventional dilution picture. The Curie-Weiss law fits reveal positive Curie temperatures in both diluted samples, indicating the persistence of ferromagnetic correlations. The robustness of the magnetocaloric response upon moderate dilution highlights the potential of YbGG-based materials for low-temperature magnetic cooling applications, particularly in addressing thermal conductivity challenges through the chemical substitution without compromising cooling power.

</details>


### [156] [Visualizing spin-polarization of an altermagnet KV$_2$Se$_2$O via spin-selective tunneling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21969)
*Guofei Yang,Chuang Li,Chengwei Wang,Xudong Zhao,Yifan Wan,Hengrui Gui,Guoqing Zeng,Saizheng Cao,Chuqiao Hu,Dong Chen,Yu Liu,Yu Song,Fei Liu,Lun-Hui Hu,Lin Jiao,Huiqiu Yuan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: KV2Se2O被确认为金属性d波交变磁体，通过自旋选择性扫描隧道显微镜直接观测到对称性保护的动量依赖自旋劈裂，遵循d波形因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁性作为新发现的磁性相，结合了零净磁化和动量依赖自旋劈裂，挑战了传统铁磁与反铁磁二分法。目前缺乏直接实验证据连接晶体对称性、电子结构和d波自旋极化。

Method: 使用拓扑绝缘体尖端驱动的自旋选择性扫描隧道显微镜，对KV2Se2O进行可视化研究，揭示对称性保护的动量依赖自旋劈裂。

Result: 在KV2Se2O中观测到特征性的d波形因子自旋劈裂，确认其为金属性d波交变磁体，建立了晶体对称性、电子结构与自旋极化之间的直接联系。

Conclusion: KV2Se2O成为研究自旋-谷锁定、费米面不稳定性和非常规磁性相互作用的可调平台，为无净磁化的对称性工程自旋电子学开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Altermagnetism, a recently identified magnetic phase that combines vanishing net magnetization with momentum-dependent spin splitting, challenges the conventional dichotomy between ferromagnets and antiferromagnets. While several candidate materials have been proposed, direct experimental evidence linking crystal symmetry, electronic structure and d-wave spin polarization remains scarce. Here we report the visualization of a metallic d-wave altermagnet in KV2Se2O. Through spin-selective scanning tunneling microscopy powered by a topological insulator tip, we uncover symmetry-protected momentum-dependent spin splitting that follows a characteristic d-wave form factor. Our results establish KV2Se2O as a tunable platform to study the interplay between spin-valley locking, Fermi-surface instability and unconventional magnetism, and open a pathway toward symmetry-engineered spintronics without net magnetization.

</details>


### [157] [In-plane and out-of-plane electric dipoles and phase transitions in 2D-layered TlGaS2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21994)
*A. D. Molchanova,L. H. Yin,L. P. Gao,W. H. Song,Y. P. Sun,K. R. Allahverdiyev,M. N. Popova*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 二维TlGaS2晶体中同时存在面内和面外电极化，表现出量子顺电性，并在约120K和60-75K温度附近观察到结构相变迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二维铁电材料中面内和面外电极化的共存现象，这对于铁电器件设计具有重要意义，因为这两种极化方向在器件中具有不同的优势。

Method: 使用二维范德华层状TlGaS2单晶，通过介电谱和红外光谱测量，详细研究量子顺电软模行为，分析Tl1+离子的偏心位移与电极化的关联。

Result: 在TlGaS2晶体中观察到面内和面外方向的量子顺电性；介电和红外光谱在约120K和60-75K温度附近出现异常，表明存在局域或弱长程结构相变；Tl1+离子的6s2孤对电子偏心位移与电极化密切相关。

Conclusion: 该研究为阐明二维层状TlGaS2中的相变以及面内和面外电极化的共存提供了重要实验证据，有助于理解二维铁电材料中的复杂极化行为。

Abstract: Out-of-plane and in-plane electric polarization, which rarely coexist in a two-dimensional (2D) ferroelectric material, offer different advantages in ferroelectricity-based devices. Here, we report the coexistence of in-plane and out-of-plane electric dipoles, along with various phase transitions, in 2D van der Waals layered TlGaS2 single crystal. Quantum paraelectricity was observed along both in-plane and out-of-plane directions of the TlGaS2 crystal. Detailed investigation of the quantum paraelectric soft-mode behavior reveals a close correlation between the electric dipoles and the off-center displacement of Tl1+ ions with 6s2 lone pairs in TlGaS2. Anomalies near temperatures of about 120 K and 60-75 K in dielectric and/or infrared spectra indicate the existence of local or weak long-range structural transitions in TlGaS2. Our results provide important experimental evidence for elucidating the phase transitions and coexistence of in-plane and out-of-plane electric dipoles in 2D layered TlGaS2.

</details>


### [158] [Interlayer-coupling-driven stabilization and superconductivity in bilayer CoTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22101)
*Wenping Chen,Ziyun Zhang,Feipeng Zheng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 层间耦合调控少层CoTe₂的结构稳定性与超导性：单层存在动力学不稳定性，而双层通过层间耦合实现结构稳定并产生约4.7K的声子介导超导


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究层间耦合对少层六方CoTe₂的影响，特别是其对晶格稳定性和量子相（如超导性）的调控作用，目前尚不清楚

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，系统研究单层和双层CoTe₂的电子结构、晶格动力学和电子-声子耦合，重点分析层间相互作用驱动的机制

Result: 单层CoTe₂在低温下表现出显著的动力学不稳定性，而层间耦合稳定了双层晶体结构，并产生声子介导的超导性，预测临界温度约4.7K；稳定性和超导性主要归因于层间耦合诱导的Te-pz电荷重分布及其对费米面和电子-声子耦合的修改；自旋轨道耦合会削弱电子-声子耦合并抑制超导性

Conclusion: 层间耦合能够协同调控少层CoTe₂的结构稳定性和超导性，为工程化调控层状过渡金属二硫属化物的量子相提供了重要见解

Abstract: Interlayer coupling plays a critical role in van der Waals materials by governing lattice stability and emergent quantum phases, yet its impact on few-layer hexagonal CoTe$_2$ remains unclear. Here, using first-principles calculations, we systematically investigate monolayer and bilayer CoTe$_2$ with an emphasis on their electronic structures, lattice dynamics, and electron-phonon coupling, and elucidate the underlying mechanisms driven by interlayer interactions. Our results show that monolayer CoTe$_2$ exhibits pronounced dynamical instability at low temperatures, whereas interlayer coupling stabilizes the bilayer crystal structure and gives rise to phonon-mediated superconductivity with a predicted critical temperature of about $4.7$~K. The stabilization and superconductivity in bilayer CoTe$_2$ are primarily attributed to interlayer-coupling-induced Te-$p_z$ charge redistribution and the associated modification of the Fermi surface and electron-phonon coupling. Finally, we discuss how spin-orbit coupling in bilayer CoTe$_2$ weakens the EPC and suppresses superconductivity. Our work clarifies how interlayer coupling can jointly tune structural stability and superconductivity in few-layer CoTe$_2$, providing insights for engineering quantum phases in layered transition-metal dichalcogenides.

</details>


### [159] [Adsorption energies and decomposition barrier heights for ethylene carbonate on the surface of lithium from cluster-based quantum chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22139)
*Ethan A. Vo,Hung T. Vuong,Zachary K. Goldsmith,Hong-Zhou Ye,Yujing Wei,Sohang Kundu,Ardavan Farahvash,Garvit Agarwal,Richard A. Friesner,Timothy C. Berkelbach*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究计算了碳酸乙烯酯在锂(100)表面的吸附能和环开分解反应能垒，验证了从有限锂簇到热力学极限的校正方案，为电解质溶剂在锂金属阳极界面化学提供了高精度基准数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在为电解质溶剂在锂金属阳极界面化学提供可靠的高精度基准数据，评估不同密度泛函方法的准确性，以指导更经济计算方法的开发和应用。

Method: 采用混合密度泛函、随机相位近似、耦合簇理论和辅助场量子蒙特卡洛等高精度理论方法，通过校正有限锂簇(40-100原子)结果获得热力学极限下的数据，计算吸附能和反应能垒。

Result: 高精度理论方法在2-5 kcal/mol范围内一致，为基准数据提供可靠参考；广义梯度近似泛函(PBE)对反应能垒精度不足，而ωB97X-V泛函在锂金属阳极界面化学中表现出色。

Conclusion: 建立了从有限锂簇到热力学极限的校正方案，提供了高精度基准数据，识别出ωB97X-V作为锂金属阳极界面化学的优选泛函，为电解质溶剂研究提供了可靠的计算方法指导。

Abstract: For ethylene carbonate on the (100) surface of lithium, we calculate the adsorption energy in two binding motifs as well as the barrier height for a ring-opening decomposition reaction. We validate a scheme for producing results in the thermodynamic limit by correcting results obtained on finite lithium clusters containing only 40-100 atoms, which enables the use of hybrid density functionals, the random-phase approximation, and correlated wavefunction theories such as coupled-cluster theory and auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo. We find that the high-level theories agree to within 2-5 kcal/mol and can therefore serve as benchmarks for more affordable methods. Using our reference data, we demonstrate that generalized gradient approximation functionals, such as PBE, are not sufficiently accurate for reaction barrier heights, and we identify $ω$B97X-V as an especially promising functional for the interfacial chemistry of electrolyte solvents at lithium metal anodes.

</details>


### [160] [Universal inverse-cube thickness scaling of projectile penetration energy in ultrathin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22207)
*Alessio Zaccone,Tim W. Sirk*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 超薄薄膜在高速度冲击下表现出显著增强的穿透阻力，其厚度依赖性遵循普适的倒数立方标度律，与材料化学组成和有序度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量模拟和实验研究，但不同材料的超薄薄膜在高速度冲击下表现出显著增强的穿透阻力这一现象，缺乏统一的物理解释。

Method: 通过分析厚度依赖性，发现比穿透能量遵循普适标度律：E_p^*(h)=E_{p,∞}^*+B h^{-3}。将这种倒数立方标度归因于有限尺寸效应对有效剪切模量的修正，源于受限固体中长波长非仿射变形模式的抑制。

Result: 该标度律定量描述了多层石墨烯、氧化石墨烯和聚合物薄膜的冲击数据，揭示了纳米尺度冲击阻力的共同弹性起源。

Conclusion: 超薄薄膜的冲击阻力增强具有普适的弹性物理机制，与材料化学组成和有序度无关，为纳米尺度冲击防护材料设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Ultrathin films of widely different materials exhibit a dramatic enhancement of projectile penetration resistance under high--velocity impact. Despite extensive simulations and experiments, a unifying physical explanation has remained elusive. Here we show that the thickness dependence of the specific penetration energy obeys a universal law, $E_p^*(h)=E_{p,\infty}^*+B h^{-3}$, independent of chemical composition and degree of disorder. The inverse--cube scaling is traced back to a finite--size correction to the effective shear modulus arising from the suppression of long--wavelength nonaffine deformation modes in confined solids. The scaling quantitatively describes impact data for multilayer graphene, graphene oxide, and polymer thin films, revealing a common elastic origin for nanoscale impact resistance.

</details>


### [161] [Decoupling Precipitation and Surface Complexation during Mn(II) Removal by Biochar via Experiments and Atomistic Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22144)
*Audrey Ngambia,Anastasiia Gavrilova,Haitao Huang,Zhuodong Lyu,Ondřej Mašek,Margaret Graham,Valentina Erastova*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合实验与模拟揭示了生物炭去除水中Mn(II)的化学机制，区分了碱性沉淀与表面络合两种主要去除途径，为设计靶向Mn(II)去除的工程化生物炭提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采矿活动导致的Mn(II)污染是持续的水质挑战，但低成本吸附剂（如生物炭）去除Mn(II)的具体化学机制尚不明确，需要建立区分不同去除途径的机理框架。

Method: 采用固定床柱实验和批量实验结合原子分子动力学模拟的方法，研究不同热解温度（350°C、550°C、700°C）油菜秸秆生物炭在酸性条件（pH 4，5 ppm Mn）下的去除性能，通过pH变化、K+释放等指标分析去除机制。

Result: 高温生物炭（700°C）去除率最高（~50%），迅速提高出水pH至9，引发碱性沉淀；低温生物炭（350°C、550°C）去除率20-30%，维持近中性pH（7-7.5），K+释放表明存在显著的阳离子交换和非沉淀途径。分子模拟证实去质子化位点通过内层络合（~50%去除）和外层结合（~10%）驱动强吸附。

Conclusion: 研究建立了区分沉淀主导和表面络合主导去除的机理框架，提供了Mn(II)靶向去除的具体化学标准，使理性设计用于可持续水修复的工程化生物炭成为可能。

Abstract: Manganese(II) mobilised by mining activity poses a persistent water-quality challenge, yet the mechanisms by which low-cost sorbents, such as biochar, sequester Mn(II) remain poorly resolved. This study identifies the specific chemical drivers of Mn(II) sequestration by combining fixed-bed column and batch experiments with atomistic molecular dynamics simulations. Oilseed rape straw biochars, produced at 350\textdegree C, 550\textdegree C, and 700\textdegree C, removed 20-50% of dissolved Mn from acidic influent (pH 4, 5 ppm). High-temperature biochar achieved the greatest removal ($\sim$50%) and rapidly increased effluent pH to 9, triggering alkaline precipitation. Conversely, lower-temperature biochars removed 20-30% of Mn while maintaining a near-neutral pH (7-7.5). Enhanced \ce{K+} release in these systems indicates significant cation exchange and non-precipitative pathways. Molecular simulations confirmed that while neutral surfaces show weak Mn(II) association, deprotonated sites drive strong adsorption through inner-sphere complexation ($\sim$50% removal) and outer-sphere association ($\sim$10%). These results establish a mechanistic framework to distinguish between precipitation-led and surface-complexation-led removal. By providing specific chemical criteria for Mn-targeted sequestration, this work enables the rational design of engineered biochars for sustainable water remediation.

</details>


### [162] [Crystallographic Orientation-Dependent Magnetotransport in the Layered Antiferromagnet -- CrSBr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22157)
*Naresh Shyaga,Pankaj Bhardwaj,Rajib Sarkar,Jagadish Rajendran,Abhiram Soori,Dhavala Suri*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过系统改变偏置电流和磁场相对于CrSBr晶体学轴的取向，全面研究了磁阻效应，揭示了磁阻作为电子各向异性探针的作用，并区分了面外和面内磁场下的不同磁阻行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CrSBr作为一种二维磁性材料，因其同时存在铁磁和反铁磁序以及高度各向异性的费米面而备受关注。研究者希望通过系统研究磁阻与晶体学取向的关系，深入了解该材料的电子各向异性特性，并为未来需要电阻对磁场梯度高灵敏度的实验开辟途径。

Method: 通过系统地将偏置电流和施加的磁场沿所有三个晶体学轴取向，全面研究磁阻效应。具体包括：1）当电流沿不同晶体学方向施加且磁场垂直于样品平面时的磁阻变化；2）面内磁场下的磁阻行为，观察各向异性磁阻和磁滞现象。

Result: 研究发现：1）磁阻可作为电子各向异性的直接探针，当电流沿不同晶体学方向施加且磁场垂直于样品平面时，磁阻表现出显著变化；2）对于面内磁场，观察到伴随磁滞的常规各向异性磁阻，表明铁磁行为；3）研究提供了CrSBr中电子输运随偏置电流和磁场相对于晶体学方向取向的完整图像。

Conclusion: 该研究通过系统分析磁阻与晶体学取向的关系，揭示了CrSBr的电子各向异性特性，区分了不同磁场配置下的磁阻行为，为未来需要电阻对磁场梯度高灵敏度的实验奠定了基础，并加深了对二维磁性材料中磁电耦合机制的理解。

Abstract: Among two-dimensional magnetic materials, CrSBr has attracted considerable attention owing to its coexistence of ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic ordering, which depends sensitively on crystallographic orientation. An additional distinguishing feature of CrSBr is its highly anisotropic Fermi surface in momentum space. In this work, we present a comprehensive investigation of magnetoresistance by systematically orienting the bias current and the applied magnetic field along all three crystallographic axes. We demonstrate that the magnetoresistance serves as a direct probe of electronic anisotropy, exhibiting pronounced variations when the current is applied along different crystallographic directions under a magnetic field perpendicular to the sample plane. For in-plane magnetic fields, we observe conventional anisotropic magnetoresistance accompanied by hysteresis, indicative of ferromagnetic behavior. Overall, our study provides a complete picture of electronic transport in CrSBr as a function of bias current and magnetic field orientation with respect to crystallographic directions, thereby opening pathways for future experiments requiring high sensitivity of electrical resistance to magnetic field gradients.

</details>


### [163] [Characterizing High-Capacity Janus Aminobenzene-Graphene Anode for Sodium-Ion Batteries with Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22254)
*Claudia Islas-Vargas,L. Ricardo Montoya,Carlos A. Vital-José,Oliver T. Unke,Klaus-Robert Müller,Huziel E. Sauceda*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 使用机器学习力场和密度泛函理论研究氨基苯功能化Janus石墨烯作为钠离子电池负极材料，揭示了其独特的三阶段储钠机制和优异的电化学性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钠离子电池需要同时具备高容量、低工作电压、快速钠离子传输和机械稳定性的负极材料，而传统负极材料难以满足这些要求。因此需要开发新型高性能负极材料。

Method: 采用SpookyNet机器学习力场（MLFF）结合全电子密度泛函理论计算，在室温条件下表征氨基苯功能化Janus石墨烯（Na_xAB）中的钠存储行为。通过不同荷电状态下的模拟研究储钠机制。

Result: 揭示了氨基苯功能化Janus石墨烯的三阶段储钠机制：氨基苯基团的位点特异性吸附和Na_n@AB_m结构形成，随后是层间填充。该材料展现出0.15 V vs. Na/Na⁺的扩展低电压平台，约400 mAh g⁻¹的比容量，可忽略的体积变化，以及约10⁻⁶ cm² s⁻¹的钠扩散系数，比硬碳高2-3个数量级。

Conclusion: 氨基苯功能化Janus石墨烯是一种有前景的结构明确的高容量钠离子电池负极材料。该研究展示了基于机器学习力场的模拟在表征电极材料方面的强大能力。

Abstract: Sodium-ion batteries require anodes that combine high capacity, low operating voltage, fast Na-ion transport, and mechanical stability, which conventional anodes struggle to deliver. Here, we use the SpookyNet machine-learning force field (MLFF) together with all-electron density-functional theory calculations to characterize Na storage in aminobenzene-functionalized Janus graphene (Na$_x$AB) at room-temperature. Simulations across state of charge reveal a three-stage storage mechanism-site-specific adsorption at aminobenzene groups and Na$_n$@AB$_m$ structure formation, followed by interlayer gallery filling-contrasting the multi-stage pore-, graphite-interlayer-, and defect-controlled behavior in hard carbon. This leads to an OCV profile with an extended low-voltage plateau of 0.15 V vs. Na/Na$^{+}$, an estimated gravimetric capacity of $\sim$400 mAh g$^{-1}$, negligible volume change, and Na diffusivities of $\sim10^{-6}$ cm$^{2}$ s$^{-1}$, two to three orders of magnitude higher than in hard carbon. Our results establish Janus aminobenzene-graphene as a promising, structurally defined high-capacity Na-ion anode and illustrate the power of MLFF-based simulations for characterizing electrode materials.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

### [164] [Vertex Centrality Reconstruction in an Inverse Problem for Information Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20710)
*Yixian Gao,Songshuo Li,Yang Yang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于边界控制方法的算法，用于从子集顶点观测到的首达时间分布中重建组合图上的顶点中心性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决信息扩散建模中的逆问题：如何从部分顶点观测到的首达时间分布来重建未观测顶点的中心性

Method: 采用边界控制方法，开发直接算法计算未观测顶点中心性，并在小规模图上进行数值实现和验证

Result: 算法在小规模图上成功实现并验证，能够从观测数据中重建顶点中心性

Conclusion: 边界控制方法适用于信息扩散逆问题，提出的直接算法能有效从首达时间分布中重建顶点中心性

Abstract: We consider an inverse problem in information diffusion modeled by random walks on combinatorial graphs. The problem concerns reconstruction of vertex centrality from the distribution of the first passage times observed on a subset of vertices. We adapt the boundary control method to obtain a direct algorithm that computes the unobserved vertex centrality. The algorithm is numerically implemented and validated on small graphs.

</details>


### [165] [Propagation of Condensation via Neumann Localization in the Dilute Bose Gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20776)
*Lukas Junge*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 证明了包含谱隙的拉普拉斯算子的诺伊曼局部化不等式，通过将立方体划分为重叠子立方体族并分析相关投影算子得到


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究拉普拉斯算子的谱隙估计问题，特别是诺伊曼边界条件下的局部化性质，旨在获得定量的谱隙估计

Method: 将立方体划分为重叠的子立方体族，分析相关的投影算子，通过离散诺伊曼拉普拉斯算子得到算子不等式

Result: 获得了包含谱隙的诺伊曼局部化不等式，得到了定量的谱隙估计结果

Conclusion: 通过几何分解和算子分析的方法，成功证明了拉普拉斯算子的诺伊曼局部化不等式，为谱隙估计提供了新的定量工具

Abstract: We prove a Neumann localization inequality for the Laplacian that includes a spectral gap. This result is obtained by partitioning a cube into overlapping families of subcubes and analysing the associated projection operators. The resulting operator inequality goes through a discrete Neumann Laplacian on the lattice of boxes and yields a quantitative spectral gap estimate.

</details>


### [166] [Scattering for anisotropic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21113)
*Evgeny Korotyaev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究非椭圆算子H₀与各向异性势V的散射问题，证明波算子的存在性与完备性，分析H的谱性质


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非椭圆算子与各向异性势的散射理论，扩展传统椭圆算子的散射理论框架，处理更一般的算子扰动问题

Method: 在H₀和V满足特定条件下，证明波算子的存在性与完备性，分析H的谱结构（无奇异连续谱，特征值仅能向0累积）

Result: 证明波算子存在且完备，H无奇异连续谱，特征值仅能向0累积；对更强的V条件，H仅有有限个特征值；结果应用于不变性原理和含时势

Conclusion: 建立了非椭圆算子与各向异性势的散射理论框架，获得了波算子的存在性与完备性，并分析了谱性质，结果可推广到不变性原理和含时势情形

Abstract: We consider the scattering for the operator $H=H_o+V$, where the unperturbed operator $H_o$ is not assumed to be elliptic and the potential $V$ is anisotropic. Under some conditions on $H_o$ and $V$ we show that the wave operators for $H_o, H$ exist and are complete, $H$ has no singular continuous spectrum and the eigenvalues of $H$ can accumulate only to zero. For stronger conditions on $V$ the operator $H$ has finite number of eigenvalues only. Moreover, these results are applied to the invariance principle and for time-dependent potentials.

</details>


### [167] [The non-uniform electron gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21200)
*Mihaly A. Csirik,Andre Laestadius*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过大正则Levy-Lieb泛函和严格相关电子泛函，为量子/经典非均匀电子气提供了严格定义，并建立了热力学极限框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Lewin、Lieb和Seiringer近期工作的启发，本文旨在为密度泛函理论早期作为理论工具的非均匀电子气提供严格定义，将其从梯度近似的构造工具提升为具有明确热力学极限的物理系统。

Method: 使用大正则Levy-Lieb泛函定义量子非均匀电子气，使用大正则严格相关电子泛函定义经典非均匀电子气，通过任意晶格周期背景密度引入非均匀性，并建立这些系统的严格热力学极限。

Result: 成功定义了量子和经典非均匀电子气系统，证明了它们作为热力学极限的存在性，并分析了这些系统的基本性质，为非均匀电子气提供了严格的数学框架。

Conclusion: 本文为非均匀电子气提供了基于大正则泛函的严格定义和热力学极限框架，将这一传统理论工具提升为具有明确数学基础的物理系统，为密度泛函理论和多体量子力学研究提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: The non-uniform (or inhomogeneous) electron gas has received much attention in many-body quantum mechanics and quantum chemistry in the early days of density functional theory, mainly as a theoretical device to construct gradient approximations via linear response theory. In this article, motivated by the recent works of Lewin, Lieb and Seiringer, we propose a definition of the quantum (resp. classical) non-uniform electron gas through the use of the grand-canonical Levy-Lieb functional (resp. the grand-canonical strictly correlated electrons functional), establish these systems as rigorous thermodynamic limits and analyze their basic properties. The non-uniformity of the gas comes from an arbitrary lattice-periodic background density.

</details>


### [168] [Limit shapes and harmonic tricks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21255)
*Nikolai Kuchumov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统阐述二聚体模型中的切平面法，并将其扩展到多连通区域，首次用椭圆函数显式参数化了带孔Aztec钻石的冻结边界


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文有两个主要动机：1) 为二聚体模型中的切平面法提供自包含的阐述，该方法是Kenyon和Prause(2020)提出的用于分析北极曲线和极限形状的技术；2) 将该方法扩展到多连通区域，特别是计算带孔Aztec钻石的冻结边界

Method: 采用切平面法分析二聚体模型，通过非平凡计算将方法扩展到多连通区域，利用椭圆函数对带孔Aztec钻石的冻结边界进行参数化

Result: 首次获得了用椭圆函数显式参数化的多连通区域北极曲线族，该族由高度变化(孔高度)索引，并推导和可视化了相应的极限高度函数

Conclusion: 本文成功扩展了切平面法到多连通区域，为带孔Aztec钻石提供了第一个椭圆函数参数化的北极曲线族，推进了极限形状理论在多连通区域的应用

Abstract: This article has two main goals. First, it provides a self-contained exposition of the tangent plane method for the dimer model - a technique for analyzing arctic curves and limit shapes introduced by R. Kenyon and I. Prause (2020). Second, it extends this method to multiply connected domains through a nontrivial computation of the frozen boundary for the Aztec diamond with a hole. This computation yields the first explicit parametrization in terms of elliptic functions of a family of arctic curves of a multiply-connected region indexed by the height change (hole height). We also derive and visualize the corresponding limit height functions.

</details>


### [169] [Homogenization of point interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21400)
*Domenico Cafiero,Michele Correggi,Davide Fermi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究非相对论量子粒子在大量点状奇异零程势作用下的均匀化极限，证明当势点密度趋于无穷时，系统收敛到具有规则静电势的薛定谔算子


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子粒子与大量点状奇异势相互作用的均匀化问题，探索当势点数量趋于无穷、间距趋于零时，离散奇异势系统如何收敛到连续规则势系统

Method: 采用Γ-收敛方法分析相关二次型的收敛性，利用强解算子收敛证明离散奇异势系统收敛到具有规则静电势的薛定谔算子

Result: 证明了当奇异势具有负散射长度且均匀分布时，随着势点数量N→∞，算子族强解算子收敛到具有规则静电势的薛定谔算子；在有外部囚禁势时，收敛提升为一致解算子收敛

Conclusion: 大量点状奇异零程势在均匀化极限下可以有效地用连续规则静电势描述，为量子系统中离散奇异相互作用的连续近似提供了严格数学基础

Abstract: We consider a non-relativistic quantum particle in $\mathbb{R}^d$, $d=2$ or $d = 3$, interacting with singular zero-range potentials concentrated on a large collection of points. We analyze the homogenization regime where the intensities of the singular potentials and the distances between the points simultaneously go to zero as their number grows, while the total interaction strength remains finite. Assuming that the singular potentials have negative scattering lengths and are uniformly distributed, we prove the strong resolvent convergence as $N \to \infty$ of the family of operators to a Schrödinger operator with a regular electrostatic potential. The result is obtained via $Γ$-converge of the associated quadratic forms. Moreover, in presence of an external trapping potential, the convergence is lifted to uniform resolvent sense.

</details>


### [170] [Dimensional analysis with constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21527)
*Umpei Miyamoto*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于线性代数的量纲分析框架，用于处理具有约束的系统，特别是变量众多或存在隐式关系的情况，通过将对数变量中的量纲关系和约束转化为线性结构，提供系统化的无量纲量计数和消除方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量纲分析在处理具有约束的系统时面临挑战，特别是当变量众多或存在隐式关系时，直接消除变量变得不切实际。需要一种系统化的数学框架来简化这一过程。

Method: 将对数变量中的量纲关系和约束表达为线性结构，将问题转化为线性代数问题。通过这种表述，可以计算独立无量纲量的数量，并提供纯代数程序来消除冗余量，无需试错。

Result: 该方法特别适用于具有隐式或多重约束的系统，能够有效处理传统方法难以应对的复杂情况。以经典阻力问题为例进行了说明。

Conclusion: 提出的线性代数框架为具有约束系统的量纲分析提供了系统化的数学工具，简化了复杂系统的无量纲量识别和消除过程，提高了分析效率和准确性。

Abstract: We develop a linear-algebraic framework for dimensional analysis in systems with constraints, particularly when variables are numerous or related by implicit relations so that direct elimination is impractical. By expressing both dimensional relations and constraints in logarithmic variables, the problem is reduced to a linear structure. This formulation yields a simple count of independent dimensionless quantities and, more importantly, a purely algebraic procedure to eliminate redundant ones without trial and error. The method is especially effective for systems with implicit or multiple constraints, and is illustrated with the classical drag force problem.

</details>


### [171] [The Spectral Shift Function for Non-Self-Adjoint Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21773)
*Vincent Bruneau,Nicolas Frantz,François Nicoleau*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究非自伴扰动下自伴算子的谱位移函数，扩展了Lifshits-Kre__n迹公式到非自伴情形，分析了谱奇异性，并应用于三维薛定谔算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受散射理论应用驱动，研究非自伴扰动下自伴算子的谱位移函数，旨在理解非自伴算子谱特性及其与散射过程的联系。

Method: 考虑迹类和相对迹类扰动，在谱和预解式适当假设下扩展Lifshits-Kre__n迹公式到非自伴算子，使用泛函演算推广谱位移函数，分析谱奇异性作用。

Result: 建立了非自伴扰动下谱位移函数的理论框架，证明了广义迹公式，展示了谱位移函数携带复特征值信息，并将结果应用于三维短程复值势薛定谔算子。

Conclusion: 成功将谱位移函数理论推广到非自伴扰动情形，为研究非自伴算子谱特性提供了新工具，并通过玩具模型验证了谱位移函数对复特征值的探测能力。

Abstract: This paper is devoted to the definition and analysis of the spectral shift function (SSF) associated with non-self-adjoint perturbations of self-adjoint operators. Motivated by applications in scattering theory, we consider both trace-class and relatively trace-class perturbations. We extend the Lifshits-Kre__n trace formula to non-self-adjoint operators under suitable assumptions on the spectrum and the behavior of the resolvent. The role of spectral singularities is carefully analyzed, and we provide a generalization of the SSF using functional calculus. Finally, we apply our results to Schr{ö}dinger operators with complex-valued short-range potentials in dimension three. Toy models illustrate properties that one might hope to extend to general cases. In particular, they suggest that the SSF carries information on the presence of complex eigenvalues.

</details>
